### Slide 38

**Text**

Retail media / Retail media may have taken 10% share in 2022 / Share of US advertising spending / Other digital / Retail / Social / Search / 75% / 50% / 25% / 0% / 2010 / 2011 / 2012 / 2013 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / Source: Zenith, GroupM / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 39

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide split into a light grey top band (title and subtitle) and a white middle band holding a full-width stacked column chart, with a light grey footer band at the bottom. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 20.5% with the chart subtitle aligned to the same left edge; the legend sits in the left margin at x 3-5%. Source line is left-aligned at x 20.5% and the author, date and slide number are right-aligned at the right margin near x 94-96%, all on one baseline in the footer at y 91%.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart, type vertical stacked columns, 13 columns for 2010 to 2022. Chart title: Share of US advertising spending. Y axis: percentage of US advertising spending, labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, range 0% to 75%, faint dotted horizontal gridlines at 25%, 50%, 75%, solid thin grey baseline at 0%. X axis: years 2010 to 2022, one label per column. Series from bottom to top: Search (black), Social (dark grey), Retail (red), Other digital (mid-light grey). No values are printed on the slide, so the following totals and segments are estimates: 2010 about 17% total (Search about 11.5, Social about 0.5, Other digital about 5); 2011 about 18% (Search about 12.5, Social about 1, Other digital about 4.5); 2012 about 20.5% (Search about 13.5, Social about 1, Other digital about 6); 2013 about 23.5% (Search about 14, Social about 2, Other digital about 7.5); 2014 about 26% (Search about 15, Social about 3, Other digital about 8); 2015 about 30% (Search about 15.5, Social about 4.5, Other digital about 10); 2016 about 34% (Search about 17, Social about 7, Other digital about 10); 2017 about 39.5% (Search about 19.5, Social about 9, Other digital about 11); 2018 about 45.5% (Search about 21, Social about 11.5, Other digital about 13); 2019 about 49.5% (Search about 22.5, Social about 13.5, Other digital about 13.5); 2020 about 57% (Search about 24, Social about 17, Retail about 2.5, Other digital about 13.5); 2021 about 64% (Search about 27.5, Social about 19, Retail about 5, Other digital about 12.5); 2022 about 67% (Search about 28.5, Social about 16.5, Retail about 9.5, Other digital about 12). Legend: vertical list at left, top to bottom Other digital (light grey swatch), Retail (red swatch), Social (dark grey swatch), Search (black swatch), swatches about 1.2% wide, text 1.85% high. Highlighted points: the red Retail segment in 2020, 2021 and 2022 is the only colour in the chart and draws the eye. Segments are separated by thin white borders. Columns are about 4% of slide width each with square corners and no shadows.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 top and bottom bands with a white #FFFFFF middle band spanning y 20.5% to 86.1%.

### Slide 39

**Text**

Advertising? Marketing? Price discrimination?
There are lots of different terms to describe the ancient practice of ‘brands paying retailers’
One estimate is that trade dollars comprise about 7.5 percent of U.S. retailers’ revenues—about two times retailers’ pre-tax income.49
According to one source, “Retailers are hooked on the heroin of this promotional money, and even the few who say they need to wean themselves from these payments know it is a difficult and arduous process. Manufacturers would love to find a way to reduce these payments…but, on the other hand, they’re used to a system that allows them to virtually buy real estate in the store.”52
Amazon ads:
7.5% of 2022
retail
revenue
Source: Competing in Tough Times, Barry Berman (2010), Amazon
Benedict Evans
–– February 2023
40

**Layout**

Single content area on a light grey field: the title sits top-left at about 20.5% from the left edge and 5.6% from the top, with a one-line subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned to the same edge. A white book-page image sits left of centre (about 19% to 69% across, 33% to 71% down) with a red circle to its right (about 77% to 94% across, 37% to 67% down); the source line sits bottom-left at the title's left edge and a footer (name, date, page number) sits bottom-right, ending near the right margin at about 95%. No slide number box is separate from the footer; the number 40 is the footer page number.

**Visuals**

One photograph of a printed book page, shown as a flat white card with a soft grey drop shadow, not full-bleed, not cropped. The page shows two paragraphs of black serif body text on white with two red underlines: a red rule beneath “7.5 percent of” at the end of the first line and a red rule beneath “U.S. retailers’ revenues” on the second line. A thin white band overlays the image region at about y 43.3% to 44.5%, covering part of the left-side text line spacing. Lighting is flat and even; dominant colours are white and black. One red filled circle (#EB483F) with white text reading “Amazon ads: / 7.5% of 2022 / retail / revenue” acts as a callout; it is a solid circle with no outline or shadow.

**Design**

Background is a flat light grey (#F1F1F1) with no gradient or pattern. Titles and subtitle are in a grey serif (#545454), with the title in MessinaModern-Regular at about 5.6% of slide height (a regular-weight transitional serif, roughly 0.7 cap height) and the subtitle at about 2.6%. Body text in the circle is white MessinaModern-Book at about 3.0% of slide height. Source and footer text are sans-serif MessinaSans: source in #545454 regular at about 1.9%, footer name bold and date/page number in light grey #A6A6A6 at about 1.9%. Accent colour is red #EB483F used for the circle and underlines. Shapes have no rounded corners; the white card has a soft drop shadow.

**Text**

Chinese suppliers going direct on Amazon / Chinese manufacturers bypass US retailers and brands and go direct to the consumer / Share of Amazon Marketplace Top Sellers based in China / 50% / 40% / 30% / 20% / 10% / 0% / Mar-17 / Sep-17 / Mar-18 / Sep-18 / Mar-19 / Sep-19 / Mar-20 / Sep-20 / Mar-21 / Sep-21 / Source: MarketplacePulse / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 41

**Layout**

Full-bleed light grey (#F1F1F1) slide with a white band across the middle, forming a header strip, a central chart area and a grey footer strip. The title is left-aligned at the top of the header with the subtitle beneath it, both starting at about x 20.5%. The chart occupies the white band with its plot area spanning roughly x 17.5% to 95% and y 35% to 80%. The source line sits at the left of the footer at about x 20.5%, and the author, date and page number sit at the right of the footer, ending at about x 96%.

**Visuals**

One line chart, a smoothed red line with no markers. Chart title: 'Share of Amazon Marketplace Top Sellers based in China' (above the plot, left-aligned). Y axis: percentage share, labelled 0% to 50% in 10% steps, gridlines at each 10%. X axis: time in half-year steps, labelled Mar-17, Sep-17, Mar-18, Sep-18, Mar-19, Sep-19, Mar-20, Sep-20, Mar-21, Sep-21. The slide prints no data values, so the values below are estimates marked about: Mar-17 about 17%, Sep-17 about 22%, Mar-18 about 24%, Sep-18 about 26%, Mar-19 about 27%, Sep-19 about 31%, Mar-20 about 33%, Sep-20 about 40%, Mar-21 about 39.5%, Sep-21 about 38%, final point about 37%. Peak near late 2020 about 42.5%. Single series, no legend. No highlighted points. Line colour is red approximately #E84C3D, about 3 px stroke.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a full-width white #FFFFFF band from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Flat, minimal, editorial look. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1 with the central band white #FFFFFF. Title text in dark grey #545454, a serif modern face (Messina Modern Regular) at roughly 5.6% of slide height, regular weight. Subtitle in the same serif, about 2.6% of slide height, #545454. Chart title and axis labels in a sans-serif (Messina Sans), regular and semibold, about 1.9% to 2.2% of slide height, colour #545554. Source line in sans-serif #545454. Footer author name in bold sans #A6A6A6, date and page number in book weight #A6A6A6. Gridlines are thin dotted grey lines. The x-axis baseline is a solid thin grey line. Accent colour is red for the data line. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Two-band layout on a light grey field: a grey header band (top 20.5%) holds the title and subtitle, a white panel fills the middle (20.5% to 86.1%) holding a single bar chart, and a grey footer band (bottom 13.9%) holds the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. Title, subtitle, chart title and source line share a left edge at about x 20.5%; the legend sits outside that margin at x 4.8%, left of the y-axis labels. The page number 43 is right-aligned at about x 95.6%.

**Visuals**

Chart: clustered vertical bar chart, titled 'Retail GMV ($bn)' (top-left above the plot). Y-axis: 0 to 500, gridlines and labels at 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, with dotted light-grey horizontal gridlines; unit $bn. X-axis: years 2015 to 2022 with labels beneath each bar pair. Series 1, Amazon Marketplace, dark grey #535353 bars; about values: 2015 about 78, 2016 about 105, 2017 about 139, 2018 about 173, 2019 about 212, 2020 about 311, 2021 about 389, 2022 about 435 (no values printed on the slide). Series 2, Shopify, red #EB483F bars; about values: 2015 about 7, 2016 about 15, 2017 about 26, 2018 about 41, 2019 about 61, 2020 about 118, 2021 about 175, 2022 about 197 (no values printed). Bars are paired per year with a small white gap between the two bars, Amazon on the left and Shopify on the right. Legend at top-left of the chart: dark grey square with 'Amazon Marketplace' above a red square with 'Shopify', stacked vertically, no border. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for the header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF for the central chart panel. Title in a serif face (MessinaModern-Regular, a transitional-style serif) in grey #545454, title cap height about 5.6% of slide height, regular weight. Subtitle in the same serif, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title and legend in MessinaSans-SemiBold/Regular, #535353, about 1.9-2.2% of slide height. Axis labels in MessinaSans-Regular #545454, about 1.85%. Source line in MessinaSans-Regular #545454. Footer author name in MessinaSans-Bold #A6A6A6, date in MessinaSans-Book #A6A6A6, page number MessinaSans-Book #A6A6A6. Accent colour red #EB483F; bars dark grey #535353. No shadows, gradients, or rounded corners; gridlines are thin dotted grey lines; baseline is a thin grey rule.

### Slide 43

**Text**

New channels, new winners / A Chinese smartphone-only fast fashion brand overtakes the market leaders? / Worldwide relative search interest / Zara / H&M / Shein / 100 / 75 / 50 / 25 / 0 / 2005 / 2010 / 2015 / 2020 / Source: Google Trends / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 44

**Layout**

Two-band layout on a light grey field: a grey header band holds the title and subtitle, with the title left-aligned at about x 20.5% and the subtitle directly beneath it. A white panel fills the middle band (y 20.5% to 86.1%) and holds a full-width line chart whose legend sits at the far left (x about 4.8%) and whose plot runs from about x 20% to 95%. The grey footer band below holds the source line at left (x 20.5%) and the author, date and page number right-aligned near x 94%. There is no logo and no slide-number field beyond the printed number 44.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'Worldwide relative search interest'. Y axis: relative search interest, unitless index, scale 0 to 100 with labelled gridlines at 0, 25, 50, 75 and 100, drawn as thin dotted light grey horizontal lines. X axis: calendar years 2005 to about 2023 with labelled ticks at 2005, 2010, 2015 and 2020, and a solid grey baseline at 0. Three series, monthly values. Zara (dark grey #4D4D4D line, about 2.5 px): starts about 13 in 2005, rises to about 25 by 2009, jumps to about 43 in late 2010, about 50 to 65 through 2012 to 2014, about 60 to 77 in 2015 to 2017, peaks at 100 about late 2007 (plotted near 2018 on the axis), typically 60 to 85 from 2018 to 2022 with a low near 50 in early 2020, and ends about 72 in early 2023. H&M (light grey #A6A6A6 line, about 2.5 px): about 11 in 2005, about 20 by 2007, about 25 to 35 in 2008 to 2011, spike to about 57 in 2012, mostly about 30 to 45 from 2013 onward, dip to about 25 in early 2020, and ends about 30 in early 2023. Shein (red #E8453C line, about 2.5 px): first appears about 1 in mid 2015, stays about 1 to 2 until 2016, rises to about 10 by 2018, about 15 to 20 in 2019, about 45 to 48 in early 2020, about 33 in late 2020, climbs to about 60 to 65 in 2022, peaks about 84 in early 2023 and ends about 75. Legend: Zara with dark grey short line, H&M with light grey short line, Shein with red short line, listed top to bottom at the left of the plot. No points are highlighted with markers.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide, with a white #FFFFFF panel across the middle band. Flat, minimal editorial style with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Title is a high-contrast serif in MessinaModern-Regular at about 5.6% of slide height, colour #545454. Subtitle is the same serif at about 2.6%, colour #545454. Body, chart labels and source line are a geometric grotesque sans, MessinaSans-Regular, about 1.85% of slide height, colour #535353 to #545454. Chart heading is MessinaSans-SemiBold at about 2.2%, colour #545554. Footer author name is MessinaSans-Bold and the date and page number are MessinaSans-Book, all about 1.85% in #A6A6A6. Accents are the series colours: dark grey #4D4D4D for Zara, light grey #A6A6A6 for H&M and red #E8453C for Shein. Header and footer band colour #F1F1F1, middle panel #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

A full-width header band (top 20.5% of the slide) sits on a light grey field, with the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.5% and stacked near the top. Below it a white content panel (y 20.5% to 86.1%) holds the chart title, a legend at the far left (x 4.8%) and a line chart spanning x 18.5% to 94.6%. A grey footer strip below the panel holds the source line at left (x 20.5%) and the author, date and page number right-aligned to about x 94.6%.

**Visuals**

One line chart titled 'Global retail revenue ($bn)'. Y axis: $bn, range 0 to 40, labelled ticks at 0, 10, 20, 30, 40 with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled tick. X axis: years 2010 to 2022, one label per year, baseline is a thin grey solid line at y 0. Legend (top-left of chart, stacked vertically): dark grey line 'Inditex (Zara)', light grey line 'H&M', red line 'Shein'. Series (values read from the chart, all approximate, no values printed): Inditex (Zara), about 13.7 in 2010, 15.1 in 2011, 17.4 in 2012, 18.2 in 2013, 19.7 in 2014, 22.8 in 2015, 25.4 in 2016, 27.7 in 2017, 28.5 in 2018, 30.8 in 2019, 22.2 in 2020, 30.0 in 2021, 35.7 in 2022. Shein, starts 2018: about 1.4 in 2018, 2.6 in 2019, 8.2 in 2020, 20.0 in 2021, 29.4 in 2022. Lines are smooth curves, no markers, no highlighted points, no data labels.

**Layout**

Three circles sit in one horizontal row across the vertical middle of the slide, with grey arrows between them, and a larger empty margin on the left (text starts at about 20% of slide width). The title sits top-left at about 5.6% of slide height, with a smaller subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on the same left edge. The source line sits bottom-left and the author, date and page number sit bottom-right on one baseline; no logo.

**Visuals**

Diagram: three filled circles of equal size (each about 22.1% of slide width by 39.3% of slide height) in a left-to-right sequence, connected by two solid right-pointing block arrows of light grey #A7A7A7 (each about 5.2% wide by 9.3% tall, sitting vertically centred between circles). Circle 1 is dark grey #535353 labelled Zara, 20-25k new products / year, 2k stores. Circle 2 is dark grey #535353 labelled Shein, 5-10k new products / day, No stores. Circle 3 is red #EB483F labelled Short-run, on-demand production and, below it, And a huge ad budget. No chart, table, photograph or icon. Circle labels are centred white text; arrows have no outline and no shadow.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the entire slide. Flat, minimal, light background #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Title in MessinaModern-Regular, a transitional-style serif, colour #545454, at about 5.56% of slide height, regular weight. Subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular, #545454, about 2.59% of slide height. Circle labels in MessinaModern-Book serif, white #FFFFFF, about 3.33% of slide height; company names Zara and Shein in MessinaModern-SemiBold, white. Footer and source in MessinaSans (sans-serif), about 1.85% of slide height: Source line Regular #545454; author name Bold #A6A6A6; date and page number Book #A6A6A6. Accent colour is red #EB483F used only on the third circle. Shapes have no rounded corners beyond true circles, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 46

**Text**

New channels + new tech = new SKUs / In the 1970s and 80s, UPCs, barcodes and computers let retailers manage vastly more SKUs / Average SKUs per supermarket, USA / 60,000 / 50,000 / 40,000 / 30,000 / 20,000 / 10,000 / 0 / 1950 / 1960 / 1970 / 1980 / 1990 / 2000 / 2010 / 2020 / Source: FMI / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 47

**Layout**

Full-width horizontal bands: a light grey header band across the top (about 0 to 20.5% of height) holding the title and subtitle, a white content area from about 20.5% to 86% holding the chart, and a light grey footer band from about 86% to 100% holding the source line at left and author, date and page number at right. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at about x 20.5%, the chart title sits left-aligned on the same left edge below the header, the chart plot runs from about x 20% to 95%, and the page number sits at the right margin near x 94% in the footer.

**Visuals**

One line chart. Title: 'Average SKUs per supermarket, USA'. Type: smoothed line chart with a single red series. Y-axis: 'SKUs' count, range 0 to 60,000, labelled every 10,000 (0, 10,000, 20,000, 30,000, 40,000, 50,000, 60,000), light dotted horizontal gridlines at each label. X-axis: years 1950 to 2020, labelled every 10 years, baseline solid thin grey line. Series values: none printed on the slide; estimates: about 3,000 in 1955, about 6,000 in 1960, about 8,000 in 1970, about 9,000 in 1975, about 14,000 in 1980, about 17,000 in 1985, about 26,000 in 1990, about 32,000 in 2000, about 40,000 in 2005, about 51,000 peak in 2008, about 47,000 in 2010, about 47,000 in 2013, about 42,000 in 2014, about 39,000 in 2016, about 30,000 low in 2017, about 36,000 in 2020. Legend: none. Highlighted points: none; the peak near 2008 and the dip near 2017 are the visible turning points. Line colour about #E8473F, stroke about 2.5 px wide, no markers.

**Design**

Background: Flat #F1F1F1 light grey, with a white rectangle covering the middle band from about 20.5% to 86% of the slide height. Clean, minimal, flat style with no shadows or gradients. Title in a serif face (Messina Modern Regular style, a transitional/modern serif) at about 5.6% of slide height, colour #545454. Subtitle in the same serif, about 2.6% of slide height, colour #545454. Chart title and axis labels in a grotesque sans-serif (Messina Sans family): chart title SemiBold about 2.2% of height, colour #545554; tick labels Regular about 1.85% of height, colour #545454. Footer text in Messina Sans: 'Benedict Evans' Bold and the date and page number in Book, about 1.85% of height, colour #A6A6A6. Accent colour for data: red about #E8473F. Header and footer bands are flat #F1F1F1; content area is flat white. Thin dotted grey gridlines about #B0B0B0.

### Slide 47

**Text**

New channels + new tech = new SKUs / Cable unlocked channel slots, and then streaming unlocked time slots / New US original TV series / Other streaming / 2,500 / Netflix / Cable / Broadcast / 2,000 / 1,500 / 1,000 / 500 / 0 / 2000 / 2005 / 2010 / 2015 / 2020 / 2022 / Source: Luminate / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 48

**Layout**

Two-band layout on a light grey field: a grey header band holds a left-aligned serif title and subtitle starting at about 20.5% from the left, and a white content panel spans the middle (about y 20.5% to 86%) with the chart. The legend sits at the far left (x about 3%) above the chart; the chart plot runs from about x 17% to 95%. The source line sits at the lower left of the grey footer band (x 20.5%), and the author, date and page number sit right-aligned in the same footer band, ending at about x 96%.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart titled 'New US original TV series'. Vertical axis: number of series, 0 to 2,500, with dotted horizontal gridlines at 500, 1,000, 1,500, 2,000 and 2,500 and labels at those values. Horizontal axis: years 2000 to 2022, one column per year (23 columns), with labels at 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015, 2020 and 2022. Four stacked series from bottom to top: Broadcast (dark grey #4F4F4F), Cable (mid grey #A7A7A7), Netflix (red #E84C3D), Other streaming (pale pink #F8DCDA). Values are not printed on the slide; approximate column totals are about 70 (2000), 85 (2001), 125 (2002), 180 (2003), 220 (2004), 250 (2005), 410 (2006), 625 (2007), 580 (2008), 735 (2009), 885 (2010), 1,120 (2011), 1,235 (2012), 1,365 (2013), 1,385 (2014), 1,430 (2015), 1,485 (2016), 1,535 (2017), 1,560 (2018), 1,590 (2019), 1,505 (2020), 1,885 (2021) and 2,020 (2022). Broadcast segments are about 60 to 210 each year; Netflix appears from about 2014 and is largest in 2019 to 2022 (about 2022: about 440 Netflix and about 500 other streaming). Legend at top left, listed top to bottom: Other streaming, Netflix, Cable, Broadcast, each with a small square swatch in its series colour. No highlighted points. Columns are separated by thin white gaps.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white panel #FFFFFF from about y 20.5% to 86%. Light grey background (#F1F1F1) with a white content panel. Title in a serif modern face (MessinaModern-Regular), dark grey #545454, large. Subtitle in the same serif, #545454, smaller. Chart title and all axis, legend and footer text in a geometric sans-serif (MessinaSans family): chart title SemiBold, labels Regular, footer author Bold and date and page number Book, in grey #A6A6A6 for the footer and #545454 for other text. Title size about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, body and labels about 1.9%. Accents are the red #E84C3D for Netflix and the pale pink for other streaming. Flat shapes, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners. Gridlines are thin dotted grey.

**Layout**

Two-band layout on a light grey field: a grey header band holds the title and subtitle, a white band in the middle holds a full-width line chart, and a grey footer band holds the source line, author, date and page number. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at about x 20.5%, with the chart title and plot sharing that left edge; the legend sits at the far left at x 4.8%. The page number '50' is at the bottom right, about x 94.3%, y 91.1%.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'US TV household penetration'. Y axis: percentage of US TV households, 0% to 100%, labelled at 0%, 25%, 50%, 75% and 100%, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each label. X axis: years 1940 to 2020, labelled every ten years (1940, 1950, 1960, 1970, 1980, 1990, 2000, 2010, 2020), with a solid grey baseline at 0%. Series 1, TV (dark grey line, about 2.5 px wide): starts about 1946 near 0%, rises steeply from about 1949 to about 80% by 1958, reaches about 90% by 1965, about 95% by 1975, about 97% by 1985, and stays flat at about 98% to 2020, ending slightly higher. Series 2, Pay TV (mid grey line): starts about 1956 near 0%, rises slowly to about 2% by 1965, about 20% by 1980, about 55% by 1990, about 75% by 2000, peaks at about 85% around 2010, then declines to about 54% by 2022. Series 3, Netflix streaming (red line): starts about 2011 at about 17%, rises to about 32% by 2014, about 45% by 2018, and ends about 56% by 2022, crossing the Pay TV line near 2021-2022. No values are printed on the chart; all readings are estimates. Legend (top left of the chart, vertical list): dark grey short line with label 'TV', mid grey short line with label 'Pay TV', red short line with label 'Netflix streaming'. No points are highlighted with markers.

**Design**

Background is light grey #F1F1F1 for the header and footer bands, with a white #FFFFFF band between y 20.5% and 86.1% holding the chart. Title 'TV unbundles…' is a high-contrast serif (Messina Modern Regular, a modern serif, close to Georgia-like) in dark grey #545454 at about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle is the same serif at about 2.6% in #545454. Chart title and all axis and legend labels are a geometric sans (Messina Sans) in regular weight at about 1.85% of slide height, colour #545454 or #535353; the chart title is SemiBold at about 2.2%. Footer source line is Messina Sans Regular #545454 at 1.85%. Footer author name is Messina Sans Bold #A6A6A6, the date and page number are Messina Sans Book #A6A6A6, all at 1.85%. Line colours: TV #545454, Pay TV #A6A6A6, Netflix streaming red #F04E37. Gridlines are thin dotted #BFBFBF hairlines. No shapes with rounded corners, shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Two-band layout: a full-width light grey header band (top 0 to 20.5%) holds the serif title and subtitle, both left-aligned to a shared left edge at about x 20.5%. A white content band (y 20.5% to 86.1%) holds the chart, with the legend at the far left (x 4.8%) and the y-axis labels, plot area and x-axis labels spanning about x 19% to 95%. No slide-number box is visible beyond the printed page number.

**Visuals**

Line chart, titled 'US TV Live+DVR hours per day' (top left of the chart, SemiBold sans). Y axis: hours per day, range 0 to 9, integer gridlines, dotted light grey horizontal gridlines at each unit, labels 0 to 9 at left. X axis: years, range 1940 to 2020, labelled every decade (1940, 1950, ... 2020), solid thin grey baseline at 0. Series 1, 'Households' (dark grey line, about #4A4A4A, about 2 px stroke, smooth): starts about 4.6 in 1949, about 4.8 in 1952, about 5.1 in 1957, about 5.0 in 1960, about 6.0 in 1970, about 6.8 in 1980, about 7.1 in 1983, about 7.0 in 1990, about 7.6 in 2001, about 8.2 in 2005, peak about 8.45 in 2009, about 8.0 in 2011, ends about 7.7 in 2014. Series 2, 'Individuals *' (red line, about #E8453C, same stroke): starts about 4.1 in 1991, about 3.9 in 1996, about 4.2 in 2000, about 4.6 in 2006, about 5.0 in 2010, about 5.0 in 2013, gap, resumes about 4.8 in 2017, about 4.5 in 2019, about 3.5 in 2021. Values are estimates from the plotted line; the slide prints no data labels. Legend at left: short dark grey line swatch with 'Households' and short red line swatch with 'Individuals *'. No highlighted points or annotations.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for the header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF for the chart band. Title and subtitle colour #545454; chart title and axis labels #545554 and #545454; footer author and page number #A6A6A6; source text #545454. Accent colours: Households dark grey about #4A4A4A, Individuals red about #E8453C. Title in MessinaModern-Regular, a modern serif, regular weight, about 5.6% of slide height, roughly 5.6% cap-plus-descender height. Subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular at about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title in MessinaSans-SemiBold, about 2.2%. Axis labels, legend and footer in MessinaSans-Regular (footer author in MessinaSans-Bold, date and page number in MessinaSans-Book), about 1.85% of slide height. Lines are thin solid strokes; gridlines are dotted light grey about #BFBFBF; baseline is solid grey about #8C8C8C. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 51

**Text**

Streaming > schlepping / Blockbuster’s peak market cap: $5bn / US home video revenue ($bn, 2022 dollars) / Tape & disc rental / Netflix total / Netflix streaming / 17.5 / 15.0 / 12.5 / 10.0 / 7.5 / 5.0 / 2.5 / 0.0 / 1980 / 1990 / 2000 / 2010 / 2020 / Source: Eberstadt, US Census, BLS, Netflix / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 52

**Layout**

Full-width three-band layout: a light grey header band (top 0 to 20.5% of slide height) holding the title and subtitle, a white body band (20.5% to 86.1%) holding the chart, and a light grey footer band (86.1% to 100%). The title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 20.5%, and the chart title and plot share that left edge. The legend sits in a left margin at x 4.8% beside the plot. The footer source line is left-aligned at x 20.5%, and the author, date and page number are right-aligned to about x 95.2%. There is no logo.

**Visuals**

One line chart titled US home video revenue ($bn, 2022 dollars). Y axis: value in $bn, range 0.0 to 17.5, labelled ticks at 0.0, 2.5, 5.0, 7.5, 10.0, 12.5, 15.0 and 17.5, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each tick. X axis: years, range 1980 to about 2022, labelled ticks at 1980, 1990, 2000, 2010 and 2020, with a thin solid grey baseline at 0.0. Legend at top-left of the chart, stacked vertically: dark grey solid line 'Tape & disc rental'; red dotted line 'Netflix total'; red solid line 'Netflix streaming'. Series 1, Tape & disc rental (dark grey solid smoothed line): starts about 1.4 in 1982, about 6.9 in 1985 to 1987, about 10.4 in 1990, about 15.2 peak in 1999 to 2000, about 15.2 peak again in 2004, about 13.2 in 2006, about 6.0 in 2011, about 3.9 in 2014, about 2.5 in 2017, and about 0.8 in 2021, where the line ends. Series 2, Netflix total (red dotted line): starts about 0.2 in 2001, about 2.5 in 2010, about 3.8 in 2012, about 6.0 in 2016, and about 9.5 in 2018, where it ends. Series 3, Netflix streaming (red solid line): starts about 2.6 in 2012, about 3.9 in 2014, about 5.8 in 2016, about 10.7 in 2019, about 12.0 in 2020, and about 14.1 in 2022, where it ends. The two Netflix series cross at about 2014 to 2015 and the tape line crosses both near 2014. No points are highlighted with markers; the line ends are unmarked.

**Design**

Background is a flat light grey header and footer band #F1F1F1 with a white central band #FFFFFF. Title text is dark grey #545454 in a high-contrast book serif (MessinaModern-Regular) at about 5.6% of slide height, regular weight. Subtitle is the same serif in dark grey #545454 at about 2.6%. Chart title and all chart and footer text use a geometric humanist sans (MessinaSans family): chart title SemiBold #545554 at about 2.2%, legend and axis labels Regular #535353 to #545554 at about 1.85%, footer source Regular #545454, author name Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number Book #A6A6A6, all at about 1.85%. Accent colour is red #E8473F for the Netflix series, and the tape series is dark grey #555555. Gridlines are light grey dotted #BFBFBF, baseline solid grey #8C8C8C. Lines are about 2 px thick. No shadows, gradients, corner radii or boxes.

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide split into three horizontal bands: a light grey header band (top 0 to about 20.5% of height) holding the left-aligned serif title and subtitle, a white middle band (about 20.5% to 86.1%) holding the chart, and a light grey footer band (about 86.1% to 100%) holding the left-aligned source line and the right-aligned author, date and page number. Content starts at a left margin of about 20.5% of width and the footer's right text ends at about 95.5% of width. The title sits top-left of the header; the chart title sits at the top-left of the white band and the chart plot spans roughly x 24% to 95%.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical grouped bar chart. Title printed above the plot: 'Content spending, 2019-2022e ($bn)'. Y axis: labelled 0 to 40 in steps of 10 (unit $bn, shown in the chart title), gridlines dotted at 10, 20, 30 and 40; axis baseline solid grey at 0. X axis: seven company groups, each with four bars: Disney, Warner Discovery, NBCU, Netflix, Paramount, Amazon, Apple. No legend and no printed data labels; the four bars per group are unlabelled and their order is not stated. Values are estimates read from bar heights: Disney about 17.5, 20.1, 25.2, 32; Warner Discovery about 19.7, 18, 22.7, 24; NBCU about 15.8, 14.7, 20.1, 22.2; Netflix about 14.6, 12.5, 17.5, 18.2; Paramount about 13.3, 13.9, 15.2, 16.4; Amazon about 5.5, 7.8, 9.9, 15; Apple about 1.8, 2.9, 6, 8. Colour coding: Disney, Warner Discovery, NBCU and Paramount bars are dark grey #535353; the Netflix, Amazon and Apple groups are red #EB4A3C. Bars are flat, touching within each group with thin white separators (about 0.1% of width), no shadows. Highlighted points: none marked beyond the red colour of the three streaming groups.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands, with a white #FFFFFF central band. Title colour grey #545454; subtitle colour grey #545454; chart title colour #545554; axis and category labels #545554; footer author and date text light grey #A6A6A6; source line #545454. Accent colour red #EB4A3C for the three highlighted groups; bars dark grey #535353. Typefaces: title and subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular, a transitional or modern serif; chart title in MessinaSans-SemiBold, sans-serif; axis labels and category labels in MessinaSans-Regular; author name in MessinaSans-Bold; date and page number in MessinaSans-Book. Title text is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, body and chart labels about 1.85%, source about 1.8%. Lines are thin dotted grey gridlines; no shape corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Full-width three-band layout: a light grey header band (top 20.5%) holding a left-aligned serif title at x 20.5% and a subtitle beneath it, a white content panel (y 20.5% to 86.1%) holding a left-side legend at x 4.8% and a line chart with its chart heading at x 20.6% and plot spanning roughly x 19.5% to 90% and y 35% to 80%, and a light grey footer band (below 86.1%) with a left-aligned source note at x 20.5% and a right-aligned author, date and page number ending at x 96%. The printed page number 54 sits bottom right; there is no separate logo.

**Visuals**

Type: line chart (three series, time-series). Chart title: Streaming subscribers (m). Y-axis: streaming subscribers in millions, linear, range 0 to 250, labelled ticks at 0, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, with dotted light grey horizontal gridlines at each labelled tick. X-axis: calendar years 2010 to 2022 labelled at each year, ticks evenly spaced, solid grey baseline at 0. Series: (1) Netflix, dark grey #545454 solid line about 2.2px, starts about mid-2011 at about 22m, about 25m in 2012, about 35m in 2013, about 47m in 2014, about 60m in 2015, about 78m in 2016, about 95m in 2017, about 120m in 2018, about 150m in 2019, about 182m in 2020, about 205m in 2021, about 222m in 2022, ending about 231m late 2022 (no values printed; all estimates). (2) Disney, red #E8473F solid line about 2.2px, starts about late 2018 at about 25m, about 31m in 2019, rises steeply to about 65m in early 2020, about 100m mid 2020, about 150m in 2021, about 200m in 2022, peaks about 236m late 2022 (estimates). (3) Core Disney+, red #E8473F dotted line about 2px, starts about late 2019 at about 28m, about 50m in early 2020, about 67m in 2021, about 85m in 2022, ending about 104m late 2022 (estimates). Legend: top-left of chart, vertical list with short line keys: dark grey solid line for Netflix, red solid line for Disney, red dot for Core Disney+. Highlighted points: none. No annotations or data labels.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 with a white #FFFFFF panel in the middle band. Light grey background with white content panel; title in a grey serif (Messina Modern style, regular weight, #545454), subtitle serif regular #545454 about half the title size; chart heading, axis labels, legend and source in a grey sans-serif (Messina Sans, regular weight, #545454 or #535353); author name bold grey #A6A6A6, date and page number book weight #A6A6A6. Accents are red #E8473F for Disney series and dark grey #545454 for Netflix. Title about 5.6% of slide height, body and labels about 1.85%. Lines are thin, no shapes with rounded corners, no shadows or gradients.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart of operating income in $bn, titled 'Operating income ($bn)'. X axis: years 2010 to 2022 printed under the axis, bars plotted at roughly half-year intervals from 2011 to late 2022, no unit printed on the axis. Y axis: -2 to 2, labelled at -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled value and a solid grey baseline at 0 and a solid axis line at -2. Series 1, Netflix, dark grey bars above zero: about 0.09, 0.10, 0.08, 0.06 in 2011; about 0.01 in 2013; about 0.04 to 0.11 across 2013.5 to 2016; about 0.14 and 0.24 in 2016.75 to 2017; about 0.43, 0.45, 0.47 and 0.21 in 2018 to 2018.75; about 0.45, 0.70, 0.97 and 0.46 in 2019 to 2019.75; about 0.95, 1.35, 1.30 and 0.94 in 2020 to 2020.75; about 1.95, 1.84 and 1.75 in 2021 to 2021.5 with about 0.62 in 2021.75; about 1.96, 1.57, 1.53 and 0.55 in 2022 to 2022.75. **Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 covering the slide, with a white #FFFFFF panel from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Subtitle in the same serif, #545454, smaller. Chart title and all axis and legend labels in MessinaSans sans-serif, regular weight, #545454 to #535353. Series colours: Netflix dark grey #545454, Disney D2C video red #E84C3D approximately. Gridlines thin dotted grey. Footer author name bold grey #A6A6A6, date and page number book weight grey #A6A6A6. ### Slide 55

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are the pale, small chart text and footers, many near-identical chart pages with repeated titles and similar subtitles (slides 22 to 25, 55 to 57), and the odd mix of data-scale labelling on slide 35, where the $502bn label sits on a bar capped at 100.

**Text**

Something missing, though? / New entrants matching legacy content producers - all new entrants / Content spending, 2019-2022e ($bn) / 40 / 30 / 20 / 10 / 0 / Disney / Warner Discovery / NBCU / Netflix / Paramount / Amazon / Apple / Source: Variety / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 56

**Layout**

Full-width header band (top 0 to 20.5% of height) in light grey holds the title and subtitle, left-aligned at x 20.5%, with the title's cap line near y 5.6%. A white content band (y 20.5% to 86.1%) holds one bar chart spanning x 18.5% to 95.5%, with the chart title left-aligned at x 20.6%, y 27.4%. A light grey footer band (y 86.1% to 100%) holds the source line at left (x 20.5%) and the author, date and page number right-aligned to x 95.5%, all on one baseline near y 91%.

**Visuals**

One grouped vertical bar chart titled 'Content spending, 2019-2022e ($bn)'. Y axis: linear, labelled 0, 10, 20, 30, 40 (no unit label on axis; unit is in chart title as $bn), range 0 to 40, with faint dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled value and a solid thin grey baseline at 0. X axis: seven categories, labels from left to right: Disney, Warner Discovery, NBCU, Netflix, Paramount, Amazon, Apple. Each category has four adjacent bars with thin white separators; the series are not labelled and no legend is printed. Values are not printed; estimated from bar heights (about): Disney about 17.5, 20.2, 25.2, 32.0; Warner Discovery about 19.7, 18.0, 22.7, 24.0; NBCU about 15.8, 14.7, 20.2, 22.3; Netflix about 14.6, 12.5, 17.5, 18.3; Paramount about 13.4, 14.0, 15.3, 16.4; Amazon about 5.5, 7.8, 10.0, 15.1; Apple about 1.9, 3.0, 6.0, 8.1. Bars for Disney, Warner Discovery, NBCU and Paramount are dark grey #535353; bars for Netflix, Amazon and Apple are red highlight. No points are individually highlighted beyond the colour split. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF for the content band. Title colour grey #545454; subtitle colour grey #545454; chart title and axis labels dark grey #545554; footer source #545454; author and date #A6A6A6. Accent red #EC4B3D for Netflix, Amazon and Apple bars; neutral bars #535353. Title typeface MessinaModern-Regular, a modern serif, regular weight, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle serif MessinaModern-Regular about 2.6%. Chart title MessinaSans-SemiBold about 2.2%. Axis and category labels MessinaSans-Regular about 1.85%. Footer author MessinaSans-Bold, date and page number MessinaSans-Book, about 1.85%. Bars are flat with no shadows, no gradients, square corners, thin white gaps between bars. Gridlines are fine dotted grey lines.

### Slide 56

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are the pale, small chart text and footers, many near-identical chart pages with repeated titles and similar subtitles (slides 22 to 25, 55 to 57), and the odd mix of data-scale labelling on slide 35, where the $502bn label sits on a bar capped at 100.

**Text**

What is ‘TV’ anyway?
YouTube creator payouts are almost certainly comparable to TV production budgets
Content spending, 2019-2022e ($bn)

57

**Layout**

Full-width three-band layout: a light grey title band across the top (about 0 to 20.5% of slide height), a white chart band in the middle (about 20.5 to 86.1%), and a light grey footer band below. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 20.5%, sharing a left edge with the chart title, the y-axis labels, the source line and the footer credit. The chart spans roughly x 20.6 to 95.2%. The source line sits at the left of the footer and the credit line, date and slide number at the right, ending at about x 95.6%. The slide number 57 is printed in the footer at x 94.4%, y 91.1%.

**Visuals**

Chart: grouped vertical bar chart. Chart title: ‘Content spending, 2019-2022e ($bn)’. Y axis: labelled 0 to 40 in $bn, gridlines at 10, 20, 30 and 40, range 0 to 40 (top gridline at about y 36.3%, baseline at about y 80.0%). X axis: eight company groups in order Disney, Warner Discovery, NBCU, Netflix, YouTube (e), Paramount, Amazon, Apple. Each group has four bars, one per year in left-to-right order, presumably 2019, 2020, 2021 and 2022e; the slide prints no legend or year labels on the bars. No values are printed on the bars, so all values below are estimates marked about. Disney: about 17.5, 20.2, 25.2, 32.0. Warner Discovery: about 19.8, 18.0, 22.8, 24.1. NBCU: about 15.8, 14.7, 20.2, 22.3. Netflix: about 14.6, 12.5, 17.5, 18.2. YouTube (e): about 10.0, 12.0, 15.8, 17.6. Paramount: about 13.4, 14.0, 15.3, 16.5. Amazon: about 5.6, 7.9, 10.0, 15.1. Apple: about 1.9, 3.0, 5.9, 8.1. Bar colours: Disney, Warner Discovery, NBCU and Paramount bars are dark grey #535353; Netflix, YouTube (e), Amazon and Apple bars are red #EB4A3C. No bars are highlighted beyond this colour split. Bars are flat with thin white gaps (about 0.5% of slide width) between adjacent bars and groups separated by wider gaps. Horizontal gridlines are fine dotted mid-grey lines; the baseline is a thin solid grey line.

**Design**

Background is light grey #F1F1F1 for the title and footer bands and white #FFFFFF for the chart band. Title text is dark grey #545454, body and axis text is dark grey #545554, the source line is #545454, and the footer credit and date are light grey #A6A6A6 with the name in bold. The accent colour is red #EB4A3C used for the four highlighted company groups. Typefaces: the title and subtitle are MessinaModern-Regular, a serif with regular weight. All other text is MessinaSans, with SemiBold for the chart title, Regular for axis labels and source, Bold for the footer name and Book for the date and page number. Title is about 5.56% of slide height, subtitle about 2.59%, chart title about 2.22%, and all axis labels, source and footer about 1.85%. Shapes have square corners, no shadows, no gradients and no borders apart from the thin gridlines and baseline.

### Slide 57

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are the pale, small chart text and footers, many near-identical chart pages with repeated titles and similar subtitles (slides 22 to 25, 55 to 57), and the odd mix of data-scale labelling on slide 35, where the $502bn label sits on a bar capped at 100.

**Text**

What is ‘TV’ anyway? / Nielsen estimates that YouTube and Netflix have equivalent viewing in the USA / Share of US TV viewing / Netflix / YouTube / 10.0% / 7.5% / 5.0% / 2.5% / 0.0% / Jun-21 / Sep-21 / Dec-21 / Mar-22 / Jun-22 / Sep-22 / Dec-22 / Source: Nielsen / Benedict Evans –– February 2023 / 58

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a light grey #F1F1F1 header band and footer band framing a white content panel. The title sits top-left at x 20.5% and the subtitle is directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a shared left edge at about 20.5% of width. The chart occupies the white panel from about 27% to 86% of height, with a legend at the far left (x 5.4%) and the plot starting at x 20.5%. The footer carries a left-aligned source line at x 20.5% and a right-aligned byline, date and page number ending at about 95.5% of width.

**Visuals**

One line chart titled 'Share of US TV viewing'. Y-axis: 'Share of US TV viewing' in percent, labelled 0.0%, 2.5%, 5.0%, 7.5%, 10.0%, range 0.0% to 10.0%, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled value. X-axis: monthly time from Jun-21 to Dec-22, labelled every three months (Jun-21, Sep-21, Dec-21, Mar-22, Jun-22, Sep-22, Dec-22), with a solid grey baseline at 0.0%. Two smoothed series: Netflix, a dark grey #545454 line, about 6.7% at Jun-21, dipping to about 6.4% in Dec-21, rising to about 8.0% near Aug-22 and ending about 7.5% at Dec-22; YouTube, a red #E8483F line, about 6.0% at Jun-21, about 5.7% near Sep-21 and Dec-21, about 6.0% in Mar-22, crossing above Netflix around Oct-22, peaking about 8.8% near Nov-22 and ending about 8.7% at Dec-22. No data values are printed on the chart; all values are estimates. Legend at top-left of the plot: dark grey short line swatch with 'Netflix', red short line swatch with 'YouTube'. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands, with a white #FFFFFF content panel. Title in MessinaModern-Regular serif, #545454, large (about 5.6% of slide height). Subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular serif, #545454, about 2.6% of height. Chart title and axis labels in MessinaSans sans-serif, #545454; chart title in SemiBold, axis and legend labels in Regular at about 1.9% of height. Footer source in MessinaSans Regular #545454; byline 'Benedict Evans' in Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in Book #A6A6A6, all about 1.9% of height. Lines are thin, about 0.3% of height, with a smooth curve; gridlines are fine dotted grey #BFBFBF-like strokes; the baseline is a thin solid grey line. No shadows, gradients or corner radii.

**Layout**

Full-bleed light grey (#F1F1F1) slide with a white band spanning the full width from y 20.5% to 86.1%, forming a middle panel between a grey header and a grey footer. The title sits top-left at x 20.5%, y 5.6%, with the subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned on a shared left edge at about 20.5% of slide width. The chart occupies the centre-right of the white band, with its legend top-left at x 4.8% and the plot starting at x 21.9%. Footer source lines are left-aligned at x 20.5% from y 90.9% to 95.4%; author, date and page number sit right-hand in the footer line at y 91.1%, with the page number at x 94.3%.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart titled 'Global 7-day hours of viewing, January 2023 (m)'. Y-axis: 'hours of viewing (m)' scale from 0 to 60, gridlines and labels at 0, 10, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60, dotted light grey horizontal gridlines. X-axis: 18 categories, no axis title, baseline solid grey line at 0. Series 1, 'Top 15 Netflix TV seasons', dark grey #535353 bars (15 bars), approximate values in left-to-right order: about 55.5, 37.5, 36, 34, 31.5, 32, 31, 30, 29, 28, 26, 25, 25, 22.5, 19, 17. Highlighted points: the three red MrBeast bars. Legend: top-left, red square and dark grey square with labels 'MrBeast YouTube' and 'Top 15 Netflix TV seasons', no border. Bar category labels 'MrBeast' with completion labels appear under the first, second and last bars only.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the full slide, with a full-width white #FFFFFF band from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Flat minimal style, no gradients, shadows or rounded corners. Legend and axis labels in MessinaSans-Regular, #535353 and #545554, about 1.85%. Footer source lines in MessinaSans-Regular #545454, about 1.85%. Author name in MessinaSans-Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in MessinaSans-Book #A6A6A6, about 1.85%. Accent colour red #EB483F for highlighted bars and legend swatch. **Layout**

Full-width light grey (#F1F1F1) band at top holds a left-aligned title and subtitle starting at about 20.5% of slide width, with a white content band below from about 20.5% to 86% of slide height and a light grey footer band beneath it. The chart title sits left-aligned at about 20.6% x, and the legend hangs in a vertical column at the far left at about 4.8% x. The source line sits bottom left at about 20.5% x, and the footer credit, date and page number sit bottom right, right-aligned to about 96% x.

**Visuals**

One line chart titled 'US teenagers’ favourite social network'. Y axis: percentage of US teenagers, 0% to 50% in 10% steps, with faint dotted horizontal gridlines at each step. X axis: years 2013 to 2022 at yearly ticks, with a thin grey baseline at 0%. Legend at left in vertical order: Instagram (solid black line), Snap (solid mid grey line), Tiktok (solid red line), Facebook (solid light grey line), Twitter (dotted light grey line). Series, all values estimated from the plotted curves (about): Instagram: 2013 about 17%, 2014 about 30%, 2015 about 29%, 2016 about 23%, 2017 about 23%, 2018 about 26%, 2019 about 35%, 2020 about 31%, 2021 about 24%, 2022 about 22%, final point about 20%. Snap: starts 2015 about 11%, 2016 about 25%, 2017 about 39%, 2017.5 about 47% (peak), 2018 about 45%, 2019 about 41%, 2019.5 about 44%, 2020 about 36%, 2021 about 31%, 2021.5 about 35%, 2022 about 31%, final point about 30%. Tiktok: starts 2020 about 13%, 2020.5 about 29%, 2021 about 30%, 2021.5 about 30%, 2022 about 33%, final point about 38%. Facebook: 2013 about 34%, 2014 about 23%, 2015 about 12%, 2016 about 15%, 2017 about 11%, 2018 about 8%, 2019 about 6%, 2020 about 4%, 2021 about 2%, 2022 about 3%, final point about 2%. Twitter: 2013 about 30%, 2013.5 about 31%, 2014 about 27%, 2015 about 21%, 2016 about 16%, 2017 about 11%, 2017.5 about 7%, 2018 about 8%, 2019 about 6%, 2020 about 4%, 2021 about 3%, 2022 about 2%. No values are printed on the slide and no points are highlighted. Lines are smooth curves with no markers; Tiktok is the only coloured series, in red.

**Design**

Background: Three flat horizontal bands: top #F1F1F1 band, white #FFFFFF content band, bottom #F1F1F1 footer band. Flat, minimal style. Title in a grey serif (Messina Modern Regular style) at about 5.6% of slide height, colour #545454. Subtitle in the same serif at about 2.6% of slide height, #545454. Chart title and all axis and legend labels in a grey geometric sans-serif (Messina Sans family) at about 1.9% of slide height, regular weight, #535353 to #545554; the chart title is SemiBold at about 2.2%. Source line in Messina Sans Regular #545454. Footer name in Messina Sans Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in Messina Sans Book #A6A6A6. Accent colour is red #E8483A (approx) used only for the Tiktok line. Lines are about 2 to 3 pt wide with round joins. Gridlines are thin dotted #BFBFBF-style grey. No shadows, gradients or corner radii.

none

### Slide 61

**Text**

But where does that happen? / Where does software eat the world, and where does it just get absorbed? / Information / arbitrage / Tickets, reviews, / listings / Software changes / the product / Uber, Airbnb… / Tesla? / Software is just / a new channel / Netflix, Shein / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 62

**Layout**

Three equal-sized circles sit in a horizontal row across the middle of the slide, spanning roughly x 19.5% to 96.2%, vertically centred near y 34% to 73%, with a grey block arrow between each pair. The title sits top-left at x 20.5%, y 5.6%, with a one-line subtitle directly beneath it at y 13.3%, both left-aligned to the same left edge. A footer line sits bottom-right at y 91.1%: 'Benedict Evans' in bold, then '–– February 2023', then the page number '62' at the far right, at about x 94.3% to 95.5%. No logo or other furniture. Large empty space lies between the subtitle and the circles and to the left of the circle row.

**Visuals**

Three filled circles and two right-pointing block arrows. Circle 1 (left): dark grey #535353 circle, label 'Information arbitrage' above 'Tickets, reviews, listings', white serif text. Circle 2 (middle): dark grey #535353 circle, label 'Software changes the product' above 'Uber, Airbnb… Tesla?', white serif text. Circle 3 (right): red #EB483F circle, label 'Software is just a new channel' above 'Netflix, Shein', white serif text. Between circles 1 and 2 and between circles 2 and 3 sits a flat light grey #A7A7A7 block arrow pointing right, with a rectangular shaft and triangular head, centred vertically on the circles. Arrows do not overlap the circles. Circles are the same size; the red circle is the only highlighted element. No chart, photograph or logo.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 with flat colour, no gradient or texture. Title and subtitle text dark grey #545454. Body text in circles white #FFFFFF. Circle fills #535353 (two) and #EB483F (one red accent). Arrow fill #A7A7A7. Footer text light grey #A6A6A6. Typefaces: title and subtitle and circle text in Messina Modern, a serif (Regular for title and subtitle, Book for circle text); footer in Messina Sans, a sans-serif (Bold for 'Benedict Evans', Book for the date and page number). Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, circle body text about 3.3%, footer about 1.9%. Circles are perfectly round with no outline, no shadow, no gradient. Arrows are flat with no outline or shadow.

none

### Slide 63

What’s the right bundle?
Nike’s own channel is now 40% of sales and all of the growth
Nike revenue* ($bn)

Benedict Evans

64

**Layout**

Two-band layout on a light grey field: a grey header band holds the left-aligned serif title and subtitle, a white chart band fills the middle, and a grey footer band holds the source line at left and the presenter, date and slide number at right. Title and subtitle start at x 20.5%, the chart and source line share a left edge near x 20.5%, and the footer text is right-aligned to about x 95.4%. A legend sits at far left (x 3.2%) outside the chart column.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked vertical bar chart titled “Nike revenue* ($bn)”. Y-axis: 0 to 50, labelled every 10, gridlines dotted grey at 10, 20, 30, 40 and 50, no axis title. X-axis: calendar years 2010 to 2022, one bar per year, solid grey baseline at 0. Two series stacked per bar: Wholesale (bottom, dark grey #535353) and D2C (top, red #EA4A3B), with a thin white separator between segments. Legend at upper left of the chart: red square labelled D2C above dark grey square labelled Wholesale. No data labels are printed; values below are estimates from the bar heights, marked about. Wholesale about: 2010 13.9; 2011 15.2; 2012 18.5; 2013 19.5; 2014 20.8; 2015 22.1; 2016 22.7; 2017 23.1; 2018 24.1; 2019 25.5; 2020 23.2; 2021 25.9; 2022 25.6. D2C segment top (total) about: 2010 16.4; 2011 18.1; 2012 21.9; 2013 23.9; 2014 26.2; 2015 28.8; 2016 30.5; 2017 32.3; 2018 34.4; 2019 37.2; 2020 35.6; 2021 42.3; 2022 44.5. No highlighted points. Bars are flat-topped rectangles with square corners and no shadows.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-bleed, with a white #FFFFFF band from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Flat, minimal style with no gradients or shadows. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1 and the chart band is white #FFFFFF. Title in a serif face (MessinaModern-Regular, a modern serif with regular weight) in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height, roughly 0.7 of that for capital height. Subtitle in the same serif, regular weight, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart heading and all chart labels in a sans-serif (MessinaSans, Regular weight for labels, SemiBold for the chart heading) in #535353 / #545554, about 1.9% to 2.2% of slide height. Accent colour red #EA4A3B used only for D2C. Footer presenter name in MessinaSans-Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in MessinaSans-Book #A6A6A6, about 1.9% of slide height. Gridlines are thin dotted grey.

### Slide 64

**Text**

The big brand bundle / Scale, efficiency and optimisation / Make it / Ship it / Promote it / Sell it / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 65

**Layout**

Left-aligned title block starts at about x 20.5% and sits near the top, with the subtitle beneath it. Four equal circles sit in a single horizontal row across the middle of the slide, joined by three grey arrows, and the footer line sits bottom right at about y 91%, with the page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Four solid circles in a row, each about 16.2% of slide width and 28.8% of slide height, with a small grey right-pointing block arrow between each adjacent pair. The first three circles are dark grey #535353 and the fourth is red #EB483F. Each circle holds a white serif label: Make it, Ship it, Promote it, Sell it. The arrows are grey #A6A6A6 block arrows, each about 8% of slide width wide and about 8.9% of slide height tall, with a rectangular shaft and a triangular head pointing right. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Flat light grey #F1F1F1 fill covering the whole slide.

### Slide 65

**Text**

The big brand bundle… unbundled
Now all the advantages of scale are in question
Outsourced
production,
short runs
New
channels,
new logistics
Social, video,
streaming
New segments,
smaller demos,
targeting
Benedict Evans
–– February 2023
66

**Layout**

Single slide with no visible grid. The title sits top-left at about x 20.5%, y 5.6%, with a one-line subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on the same left edge. A horizontal row of four equal circles sits across the vertical middle (y 39.1% to 67.9%), joined by three arrows, with scattered bubbles around them. The footer is right-aligned at the bottom right (y 91.1%), with the page number 66 at x 94.3%; no logo or other corner marks.

**Visuals**

A process diagram of four large circles in a left-to-right row, joined by three solid grey right-pointing block arrows. Circles 1 to 3 are dark grey #535353 and circle 4 is red #EB483F; each holds white serif text. Arrows are flat #A7A7A7 block arrows about 8% of slide width wide and 7% tall, placed in the gaps between circles at x about 35.9%, 55.9% and 75.9%, y about 50.3%. Around the circles sit about 21 grey #A7A7A7 solid circles of varying diameters (from about 1.7% to 4.9% of slide width) as scattered decorative bubbles, and about 8 pink-red bubbles around circle 4 in a lighter tint of #EB483F (approximately #F0A09B, one at 50% opacity), with no outlines or shadows. Bubbles are not connected to each other and overlap nothing except sitting close to the circles' edges.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat, minimal, muted look. Background light grey #F1F1F1. Title in MessinaModern-Regular (a transitional serif), regular weight, colour #545454, roughly 5.6% of slide height (about 7.8% for capital-height letters). Subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Circle labels in MessinaModern-Book, white #FFFFFF, about 3.0% of slide height, centred and set over three lines. Footer in MessinaSans, 1.85% of slide height, colour #A6A6A6; 'Benedict Evans' in Bold, the rest in Book. Accent colour is red #EB483F, used only for the final circle and its bubbles. Shapes are flat with no corner radius, shadow or gradient.

### Slide 66

**Text**

Old and new gatekeepers
What kind of gatekeeping are we talking about?
Sears stocked 200k items
Amazon lists at least
300m items and has 2m
seller accounts
2,000 new TV series
made in the US in 2022
3.7m daily uploads to
YouTube
In 1994 98% of US city
newspapers were local
monopolies
Well… the internet
Source: Sears Roebuck, NRF, Amazon, Variety, Alphabet, FCC, MarketplacePulse
Benedict Evans
–– February 2023

**Layout**

Three horizontal rows of text sit on white bands stacked under a grey header, each row split into a left statement column starting at about 20.5% of slide width, a red dotted arrow in the middle (about 49% to 65% of width), and a right result column starting at about 73.3%. The title sits top-left at about 20.5% from the left and 5.6% from the top, with a one-line subtitle beneath it; the source line and footer run along the bottom grey band, with the page number at the far right at about 94% of width.

**Visuals**

Three red dotted arrows, one per row, each a horizontal dotted line with a solid triangular arrowhead pointing right, running from about x 49.4% to 65% of slide width at the vertical centre of each row (about y 30%, 53% and 76% of slide height). Each arrow is a dotted stroke about 0.3% of slide height thick in red #E5423A. No photographs, charts, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with white #FFFFFF content bands behind the rows. Flat two-tone layout: light grey background #F1F1F1 with full-width white bands #FFFFFF for the three content rows, separated by thin grey stripes #F2F2F2. Title and body text in MessinaModern serif (Book weight for body, Regular for title) in dark grey #545454; the title is about 5.6% of slide height and body text about 3.3%, subtitle about 2.6%. Footer in MessinaSans, Regular for the source line and Book for the date, with Bold for the author name, about 1.9% of slide height, in #545454 for the source and #A6A6A6 for the author, date and page number. Accent colour is red #E5423A on the arrows only. Shapes are plain rectangles with square corners and no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 67

**Text**

A market reset
If all the old filters and gatekeepers go away, what opportunities does that create?
Infinite product
A new kind of problem
Blank canvas for new businesses
Infinite media
Benedict Evans –– February 2023
68

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about x 20.5%, with a one-line subtitle directly beneath it at the same left edge; the content is a horizontal row of three equal circles centred vertically in the lower-middle of the slide, joined by two grey arrows. Footer runs right-aligned along the bottom at about y 91% with the page number at the far right; there is no fixed left margin for the footer.

**Visuals**

Three filled circles of equal size (each about 22.1% of slide width and 39.3% of slide height, so true circles), left and right circles dark grey #535353 with white text, centre circle red #EB483F with white text. Between left and centre circle is a grey (#A7A7A7) right-pointing block arrow with a rectangular shaft about half the arrow height and a triangular head; between the centre and right circle is a mirror-image left-pointing grey block arrow. Arrows point inward toward the red centre circle. No photographs, charts or logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1, no gradient, photograph or pattern. Flat, minimal look on a light grey background. Title in a Modern serif (Messina Modern Regular), #545454, about 5.56% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif, #545454, about 2.59% of slide height. Circle labels in white serif (Messina Modern Book for left and right circles, Regular for the red circle), about 3.33% of slide height. Footer in a sans-serif (Messina Sans), #A6A6A6, about 1.85% of slide height; 'Benedict Evans' bold, the date and page number book weight. Shapes have no outlines, shadows or gradients; arrows are flat #A7A7A7 fills.

### Slide 68

**Text**

“Advertising is the price you pay for / having an unremarkable product or / service” / Jeff Bezos, 2009 / Benedict Evans –– February 2023 / 69

**Layout**

A single full-bleed light grey slide with one white rectangular panel placed right of centre, covering roughly x 17 to 96 and y 25 to 86. The quote is set flush left inside the panel near its top, starting at x 20.5 and y 30.4, with the attribution below it after a blank line. There is no title bar, no logo and no slide-level grid beyond the panel. The footer sits bottom right, aligned to the panel's right edge at about 95.6, on one line at y 91.1: author name, en-dash separator and date, then the page number.

**Visuals**

None. The slide contains no photograph, chart, icon, logo or illustration; the only graphic elements are the two flat rectangles forming the background and the white panel.

**Design**

Background: Flat light grey #F2F2F2 fill covering the entire slide. Flat, minimal look with no shadows, gradients, borders or rounded corners. Background is light grey #F2F2F2 and the panel is pure white #FFFFFF. The quote and attribution are set in a book-weight modern serif (Messina Modern Book style, a high-contrast Didone-like serif) in red #EB483F, at about 5.56% of slide height with normal leading of roughly 6.7% of slide height between lines. The footer uses a humanist-geometric sans (Messina Sans, Bold for the name and Book for the rest) at about 1.85% of slide height in mid grey #A6A6A6. Text is left-aligned with no bullets, and the quote opens with a curly double quotation mark.

70

**Layout**

Two-band slide with a full-width light grey header band holding the title and subtitle, a white middle band holding a full-width bar chart, and a light grey footer band holding the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. Content starts at about x 20.5% and the title, subtitle, chart title and source share that left edge; the chart runs to about x 95%. The page number reads 70 at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Chart: clustered vertical bar chart titled '10 largest global advertising budgets, 2017-2021 ($bn)'. Y-axis is labelled in $bn with a range of 0 to 20 and dotted horizontal gridlines at 0, 5, 10, 15 and 20; the axis tick labels are shown. X-axis shows ten company groups: Amazon, Alibaba, L'Oréal, P&G, Samsung, LVMH, Unilever, Nestlé, Alphabet, Comcast. Each group is a cluster of thin adjacent bars with no printed values, so the values below are estimates marked about. Amazon (red, five bars): about 6.3, 8.2, 11.0, 10.9, 16.9. Alibaba (red, four bars): about 3.3, 4.5, 8.5, 14.2. L'Oréal (grey, five bars): about 8.7, 9.6, 10.3, 9.9, 12.5. P&G (grey, five bars): about 10.5, 10.1, 10.6, 11.4, 11.1. Samsung (grey, five bars): about 11.2, 10.1, 9.7, 8.6, 10.1. LVMH (grey, five bars): about 5.4, 6.5, 7.4, 5.8, 8.6. Unilever (grey, five bars): about 8.5, 8.4, 8.1, 8.1, 8.1. Nestlé (grey, five bars): about 7.2, 7.3, 7.4, 7.8, 8.0. Alphabet (red, five bars): about 5.1, 6.4, 6.7, 5.3, 7.9. Comcast (grey, five bars): about 7.0, 7.4, 7.5, 6.7, 7.6. Highlighted points: the Amazon, Alibaba and Alphabet groups are red #EB483F; all other groups are dark grey #535353. There is no legend. Bars have thin white gaps between them and no outlines. The chart has no visible border and sits on the white band.

**Design**

Background is light grey #F1F1F1 for the header and footer bands, with a white #FFFFFF central band. Title is in MessinaModern-Regular, a serif with a large size (about 5.6% of slide height, cap height about 0.7 of that) in grey #545454. Subtitle is MessinaModern-Regular serif at about 2.6% in #545454. Chart title and axis labels are MessinaSans, a clean grotesque sans; chart title is SemiBold at about 2.2%, axis labels Regular at about 1.85%, colour #545554. Source line is MessinaSans Regular at about 1.85% in #545454. Footer author name is MessinaSans Bold and the date and page number are MessinaSans Book, all at about 1.85% in light grey #A6A6A6. Red accent #EB483F, dark grey data #535353. Gridlines are thin dotted grey lines. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Single left-aligned column. A translucent light panel spans from about 17% to 95.5% of the width and from about 20% to 81.5% of the height, with the title set near its top-left corner. A thin white rule runs along the bottom edge of the panel. The footer cluster sits at the bottom right, aligned to the panel's right edge at about 95.5%, with the page number at the far right and no left-side content.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Flat solid colour #36494E, dark slate teal, covering the whole slide. Flat dark slate-teal background #36494E. A white panel at 4% opacity sits over it, lightening the area to roughly #3D5055 with no border, shadow or corner radius. Title is white (#FFFFFF) in a high-contrast serif (MessinaModern Regular, a Modern-style serif, regular weight) at about 9.3% of slide height, with no capitals or letter-spacing change. Footer is in MessinaSans, about 1.85% of slide height: 'Benedict Evans' in Bold and the date and page number in Book, all in grey #A6A6A6. The divider is a solid white line about 0.3% of slide height thick, spanning the panel width.

### Slide 71

**Text**

Three steps to the future / “The future is here, but it’s unevenly distributed” - William Gibson / Past / Old economy / disrupted by tech / ideas from the / 2000s / Present / Startups deploy / ideas from the / 2010s / Future / What’s the next / fundamental / platform shift? / Benedict Evans –– February 2023 72

**Layout**

Three equal white circles sit in a single horizontal row across the centre of the slide, spaced evenly, with a grey block arrow in each gap, starting at about 19.6% from the left and ending at about 96% from the left. The title sits top-left at x 20.5%, y 5.6%, with a one-line quotation subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned on a shared left edge. Footer text is right-aligned at the bottom right (y 91.1%) with the page number at the far right; there are no rules and no logos.

**Visuals**

Three identical white (#FFFFFF) circles, each with a thin red (#E84C3D) outline about 0.3% of slide height wide, each containing a bold heading and a regular-weight text block centred inside it. Between circle 1 and circle 2, and between circle 2 and circle 3, sits a solid grey (#A7A7A7) right-pointing block arrow with a rectangular shaft and a triangular head; the arrows are not connected to the circles by lines, they sit in the gaps. No photographs, charts or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide. Flat light-grey background (#F1F1F1) with no gradient or texture. Title and body text in dark grey #545454; footer text in lighter grey #A6A6A6; accents are red circle outlines (#E84C3D) and grey arrows (#A7A7A7). Title and body are a transitional serif (MessinaModern, Regular, with SemiBold for the circle headings); the footer is a sans-serif (MessinaSans, Bold for the author name and Book for the date and page number). Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, circle headings about 3.3%, circle body about 3.0%, footer about 1.9%. Circles are perfect circles with no shadow; arrows have no outline and no shadow.

**Visuals**

Chart: clustered vertical bar chart, two series per period, titled 'Enterprise workloads in public cloud'. Y axis: percentage of enterprise workloads, range 0% to 50%, labelled ticks at 0%, 10%, 20%, 30%, 40%, 50%, with dotted light grey horizontal gridlines at each tick. X axis: 16 time periods labelled Dec-14, Aug-15, Dec-15, Jun-16, Dec-16, Jun-17, Dec-17, Jun-18, Dec-18, Jun-19, Dec-19, Jun-20, Dec-20, Jun-21, Dec-21, Jun-22, with a thin solid grey baseline at 0%. **Design**

Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1 and the central content band is white #FFFFFF. Title and subtitle are in a serif (Messina Modern Regular, a transitional serif) in grey #545454 at regular weight; title size about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%. Chart title is in a semibold humanist sans (Messina Sans SemiBold) in #545554 at about 2.2%. Axis labels, legend and source are in Messina Sans Regular at about 1.85% in #545454 or #535353. Footer credit 'Benedict Evans' is in Messina Sans Bold, the date in Messina Sans Book, and the page number in Messina Sans Book, all in light grey #A6A6A6 at about 1.85%. Accent colour is red #EB4A3C for the Today series; second series is dark grey #535353. Gridlines are dotted, light grey. No shadows, gradients, corner radii or rules other than the chart baseline.

### Slide 74

**Text**

‘Speculation as a feature’ can work both ways / Jump start the network effect by rewarding early-adopters - but are they users or speculators? / OpenSea NFT trading / Value traded ($bn) / 6 / 600 / MAU (000)* - RH scale / 5 / 500 / 4 / 400 / 3 / 300 / 2 / 200 / 1 / 100 / 0 / 0 / Mar-20 / Jun-20 / Sep-20 / Dec-20 / Mar-21 / Jun-21 / Sep-21 / Dec-21 / Mar-22 / Jun-22 / Sep-22 / Dec-22 / Source: dune.xzy @rchen / * Accounts trading at least once in the month / Benedict Evans / –– February 2023 / 75

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a light grey band-and-panel structure: a full-width grey header band holds a left-aligned serif title at about 20.5% from the left, with the subtitle beneath it at the same left edge. A white panel fills the middle of the slide (from about 20.5% to 86.1% of height) and holds a line chart whose chart title sits at the left edge at x 20.6%, with a legend at the far left (x 3% to 14%). The plot runs from about x 19% to x 98%, with left-axis labels just left of the plot and right-axis labels just right of it, and a month axis along the bottom. A grey footer band below the panel carries the source and footnote at the left (x 20.5%) and the author, date and page number at the right, ending at about x 95%.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled OpenSea NFT trading. Two series, both drawn as smoothed lines with no markers. Left axis: Value traded ($bn), linear, 0 to 6 in steps of 1, no unit label beyond $bn in the legend. Right axis: MAU (000)* - RH scale, 0 to 600 in steps of 100, numbers only. Horizontal axis: monthly time from about Jan-20 to Dec-22, labelled every three months from Mar-20 to Dec-22. Horizontal dotted grey gridlines at each left-axis step. Legend at top left of the chart: red line marker followed by Value traded ($bn); dark grey line marker followed by MAU (000)* - RH scale. Red series (Value traded, $bn), estimated values: about 0 from early 2020 to Dec-20; about 0.15 at Mar-21; about 0.1 at Apr-21; about 0.15 at Jun-21; about 0.3 at Aug-21; about 3.4 at Sep-21 (peak of first rise); about 2.9 at Oct-21; about 2.15 at Nov-21 (trough); about 2.6 at Dec-21; about 4.85 at Jan-22 (highest point); about 2.5 at Mar-22 (trough); about 3.5 at Apr-22 (second peak); about 2.55 at May-22; about 0.65 at Jun-22; about 0.55 at Jul-22; about 0.5 at Aug-22; about 0.35 at Sep-22; about 0.3 at Oct-22; about 0.25 at Nov-22; about 0.28 at Dec-22. Dark grey series (MAU (000)*, plotted on right scale 0 to 600), estimated values: about 5 at Mar-20 to Dec-20; about 5 at Mar-21 (about 50 on right scale); about 30 at Apr-21; about 25 at May-21; about 40 at Jun-21; about 60 at Jul-21 (about 100 on right scale in Aug-21 region); about 210 at Sep-21 range; about 290 at Oct-21; about 275 at Nov-21; about 370 at Dec-21; about 550 at Jan-22 (highest point); about 530 at Feb-22; about 455 at Mar-22; about 480 at Apr-22; about 430 at May-22; about 400 at Jun-22; about 415 at Jul-22; about 380 at Aug-22; about 335 at Sep-22; about 285 at Nov-22; about 290 at Dec-22. No data labels are printed on the chart; all values above are estimates. Highlighted points: none marked. No shading or fills in the plot.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF panel across the middle band. Light grey (#F1F1F1) header and footer bands with a white central panel. Title in a high-contrast serif with a neutral grey colour, roughly 5.6% of slide height per capital letter cap line; subtitle in the same serif at about 2.6%. Chart title and all axis and legend labels in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (Messina Sans style), regular weight, about 1.9% of slide height. Chart title in semibold. Body and source text in dark grey. Right-axis numbers in near-black, left-axis numbers in mid grey. Accent colours are the red series (#E84C3D) and the dark grey series (#4A4A4A). Lines are about 2 px wide at slide scale with dotted light grey gridlines. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or borders.

### Slide 75

**Text**

“Wait, how much money, Mark?”
Meta spent $14bn on ‘metaverse’ in the last 12 months and is years from a consumer launch
Quarterly budgets ($bn, 2022 dollars)

2

Apple R&D budget, 8 quarters before iPhone launch

Source: Apple, Meta, BLS

76

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a light grey header band across the top (0 to 20.5% of height) holding the title and subtitle, left-aligned at about x 20.5%. A white content area fills the middle (20.5% to 86.1%) containing a two-cluster column chart with its title above the plot at x 20.6%. A light grey footer band runs along the bottom (86.1% to 100%) with the source line at left (x 20.5%) and the author, date and page number right-aligned to about x 95.5%. No slide-number box is separate from the footer text; the page number 76 sits at the far right of the footer.

**Visuals**

Column chart titled 'Quarterly budgets ($bn, 2022 dollars)'. Y-axis: linear, labelled 0 to 5 in units of $bn (2022 dollars), gridlines dotted at 1, 2, 3, 4 and 5, with a solid baseline at 0. X-axis: two categories with labels 'Apple R&D budget, 8 quarters before iPhone launch' (left) and 'Meta Reality Labs operating loss, last 8 quarters' (right). Series 1 (dark grey #535353): eight adjacent columns for Apple R&D budget, each about 0.15 to 0.2 $bn high (no values printed; estimates only, about 0.15, 0.15, 0.18, 0.17, 0.17, 0.18, 0.18, 0.18). Series 2 (red #EB483F): eight adjacent columns for Meta Reality Labs operating loss, with no values printed; estimated heights about 1.8, 2.4, 2.6, 3.3, 2.95, 2.8, 3.65, 4.3 $bn, rising left to right with the last column highest. Columns are flush with thin white gaps between them, no rounded corners, no shadows. No legend; the two categories are identified by the axis labels. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF content band between y 20.5% and 86.1%.
