### Slide 76

**Text**

But what are the questions that matter?
Nothing that’s happened in the last 12 months gives us answers either way
Can blockchains be software?
Is xR the next smartphone?
‘DeFi’ - can you build financial
applications with a blockchain?
(This is what imploded in 2022)
Can you build actual consumer-
scale applications on a blockchain?
Could it be ‘web 3’?
The hardware will get better…
But will we care, or is this just a
subset of games consoles?
Benedict Evans
–– February 2023
77

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a full-width white band between a grey header band holding the title and subtitle and a grey footer band holding the speaker name, date and page number. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 20.5% in the header, with the left column starting at x 20.6% and the right column at x 64%, separated by a vertical dashed red divider at about x 56.3%. Footer text is right-aligned at the bottom right, with the page number at x 94.4% and about 5.6% margin to the right edge.

**Visuals**

One non-photographic element: a single vertical dashed red divider line running from about y 31.6% to y 78% at about x 56.3%, dividing the two text columns. No photographs, charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-bleed, with a white #FFFFFF band from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1; the central content band is white #FFFFFF. Title and subtitle text is grey #545454; headings and body text are dark grey #474747; footer text is light grey #A6A6A6; the divider is red, about #E74C3C. Typefaces are MessinaModern (a book-style serif) in Regular, SemiBold and Book weights for titles, headings and body, and MessinaSans in Bold and Book for the footer. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, headings about 3.3%, body about 3.15%, footer about 1.85%. Lines and shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 77

**Text**

Remember - the future can take a long time
And sometimes the early leaders disappear when it works
Smartphone unit sales (m)
Proto-smartphones*
iPhone & Android
1,500
1,250
1,000
750
500
250
0
2000
2005
2010
2015
Source: IDC, Companies
Benedict Evans
–– February 2023
78
* S60, Palm, RIM, Windows Phone

**Layout**

Full-width two-tone layout: a light grey header band holds a left-aligned serif title and subtitle, a white central band holds the line chart, and a light grey footer band holds the source lines at left and the author, date and page number at right. Title and subtitle start at x 20.5% and the chart legend sits at the left margin (x 4.8%). Page number 78 sits at the right margin (x 94.4%) in the footer.

**Visuals**

Line chart with markers, titled 'Smartphone unit sales (m)'. X axis: years 2000 to 2015, labelled at 2000, 2005, 2010 and 2015, with gridlines absent on the x axis. Y axis: unit sales in millions, range 0 to 1,500, labelled 0, 250, 500, 750, 1,000, 1,250 and 1,500 with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each label. Series 1 'Proto-smartphones*' (dark grey #545454 line, filled dark grey circle markers, smoothed curve): about 3 (2001), about 5 (2002), about 12 (2003), about 33 (2004), about 53 (2005), about 68 (2006), about 78 (2007), about 88 (2008), about 103 (2009), about 150 (2010), about 131 (2011), about 57 (2012), about 24 (2013); the series ends at 2013. Series 2 'iPhone & Android' (red #EE4035 line, filled red circle markers, smoothed curve): about 5 (2007), about 15 (2008), about 27 (2009), about 107 (2010), about 270 (2011), about 505 (2012), about 980 (2013), about 1,250 (2014), about 1,420 (2015). Legend at left, top-left of chart, stacked vertically: grey marker with 'Proto-smartphones*' above red marker with 'iPhone & Android'. No highlighted points beyond the markers. The two series cross between 2010 and 2011.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer bands with a white #FFFFFF central band. Light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF chart band between them. Title in serif Messina Modern Regular, dark grey #545454, sized about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title and axis labels in sans-serif Messina Sans, #545454, regular weight, about 1.9% of slide height. Chart title is semi-bold. Footer author name in bold sans #A6A6A6, date and page number in book weight sans #A6A6A6. Source lines in regular sans #545454. Accent colours: dark grey #545454 for the proto-smartphone series and red #EE4035 for the iPhone and Android series. Lines are about 2 px wide with filled circular markers, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners.

One photograph-style illustration: a 4 columns by 4 rows grid of 16 greyscale, blurred, computer-generated human face images, each a square tile, separated by uniform mid-grey gutters, on a mid-grey (#808080) backing rectangle with square corners. Subjects are frontal, centred faces. Tones range from very dark to near white, with some tiles noisy and low-contrast and others sharper. Top row: a dark-toned face with open mouth, a dark face with partial features, a pale face, a very pale face. Second row: a faint featureless tile, a light face smiling, a face with dark hair edges, a smiling face with dark shading. Third row: a dark-edged face with nose emphasised, a blurred face, a face with dark eyes, a smiling face with light hair. Bottom row: a bright face with open mouth, a light face with mouth, a light face with dark nostrils and mouth, a very faint face. Lighting is flat and even; the grid is fully visible, not cropped, boxed with square corners, no overlay or tint. No logos or icons.

Title and quotation text dark grey #545454 in a high-contrast serif (Messina Modern style, regular weight, not italic, no capitals, normal letter-spacing). Source line dark grey #545454 in a sans-serif (Messina Sans Regular). Footer name bold and date and number in light grey #A6A6A6 in Messina Sans (bold for name, book/regular for date and number). Title text about 5.6% of slide height; quotation about 3.0%; source and footer about 1.9%. Image grid has square corners and mid-grey #808080 gutters with no rounding.

**Text**

“film still, portrait of an old man, wrinkles, dignified / look, grey silver hair, peculiar nose, wise, eternal / wisdom and beauty, incredible lighting and camera / work, depth of field, bokeh, screenshot from a / hollywood movie”
Source: Fabian Stelzer
Benedict Evans

83

**Layout**

Two-column split: a light grey left panel occupies the left half (0 to 50% width) holding the title, quote and source line, and a white right half holds a single square photograph inset with margins of about 3.5% from the right edge and 8% from the top. The title sits vertically around the middle-left at about 25.6% from the top, left-aligned at 4.6% from the left margin, with the quote beneath it. The source line sits bottom-left at about 91% from the top. The footer (author, date, and slide number) is right-aligned in the white half at about 91% from the top, with the slide number at the far right at about 94% to 95.5% of width.

**Visuals**

One photograph, boxed (not full-bleed), placed in the right half. Subject: close-up portrait of an elderly man with deeply wrinkled skin, bushy grey and silver eyebrows, grey stubble and beard, and grey hair at the top edge, looking off to the left of frame with a pensive expression. Composition: tight crop from the forehead to the chin, the face angled three-quarters to the left, the far eye partly in shadow and the near eye in focus; shallow depth of field with the background blurred into dark charcoal at the left edge. Lighting: soft directional light from the upper right, producing warm highlights on the cheek and nose and cool shadows. Dominant colours: warm skin tones of terracotta and peach, with grey, silver and white hair and a dark grey background. The image is square, not cropped by a shape, with a faint soft drop shadow along its bottom and right edges, and no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: two-tone, light grey #F2F2F2 on the left half and pure white #FFFFFF on the right half, with no gradient or pattern. Title colour: dark grey #545454, in a high-contrast serif (Messina Modern Regular, a transitional serif) at roughly 5.6% of slide height, regular weight. Body text colour: dark grey #535353, Messina Modern Book serif at about 3.0% of slide height, regular weight, with generous line spacing of about 3.6% of slide height between lines. Source line: Messina Sans Regular, a neutral grotesque sans-serif, at about 1.85% of slide height, colour #545454. Footer: Messina Sans; author name in Bold #A6A6A6, the date in Book #A6A6A6 at about 1.85% of slide height, and slide number in Book #A6A6A6. No accent colours. Lines and shapes: none apart from the photograph, which has square corners and a subtle shadow.

write a country song about how you made lots of money from social media, and now you think social media is destroying democracy, but you aren't giving the money back
Verse 1:
Started with a laptop and a camera lens
Posting pics and vids, I was just a kid

Now I've got a bank account that's bulgin'
Chorus:
I made lots of cash from social media fame
But now I see it's tearing democracy apart in shame
I'll keep my money, it's mine to keep
Even though I know social media's ruining the world, deep
Verse 2:
Used to think it was all just fun and games
But now I see the harm, it's not just a shame
ChatGPT Jan 30 Version. Free Research Preview. Our goal is to make AI systems more natural and safe to interact with. Your feedback will help us improve.

### Slide 85

**Text**

Generative search?
How many more search queries could be served as answers? / Where do the ads go? How are they sold?
Source: Benedict Evans / Perplexity
perplexity.ai
+ New
Copy
Share
Name all of the various laws - such as / Moore's Law and Metcalfe's Law - associated / with technology
PERPLEXITY
View Detailed
Moore's Law states that the number of transistors in a dense / integrated circuit (IC) doubles about every two years[1]. Wirth's Law / states that software is getting slower more rapidly than hardware is / becoming faster[2]. Brooks' Law states that adding manpower to a / late software project makes it later[2]. Hofstadter's Law states that / tasks always take longer than expected, even when accounting for / Hofstadter's Law[2]. Segal's Law states that a man with a watch / knows what time it is; a man with two watches is never sure[2]. / Conway's Law states that organizations which design systems are / constrained to produce designs which are copies of the / communication structures of these organizations[2]. Metcalfe's Law / states that the value of a network is proportional to the square of the / number of connected users of the system[3].
Ask a follow up
Benedict Evans
–– February 2023
86

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. The left column (0 to 50% width) sits on a light grey field and holds the title and subtitle in a left-aligned stack starting at about 25.6% height, with the source line at the bottom left at about 91% height. The right column (50 to 100% width) is white and holds a browser-window screenshot boxed at x 54.1 to 95.9%, y 4.0 to 86.8%, with a footer at the bottom right: author name, date and page number 86 on one baseline at about 91% height. Left margin is about 4.6% of slide width; the title is not at the top of the slide.

**Visuals**

One photograph-style screenshot of a macOS-style browser window showing the Perplexity.ai web app, not cropped, boxed with a soft drop shadow. Window chrome: light grey title bar with red, yellow and green traffic-light dots at top left, a rounded URL field reading perplexity.ai with a padlock icon and reload icon, then a toolbar with a black square app-grid icon, blue '+ New' button, and grey 'Copy' (link icon) and 'Share' (Twitter bird icon) at top right. Content: a bold black query heading, a blue arrow icon with spaced blue caps 'PERPLEXITY' label, a 'View Detailed' link with list icon, a body paragraph with superscript citation numbers, a horizontal hairline divider, and a rounded pill input with search icon, placeholder 'Ask a follow up' and a blue circular plus button. Dominant colours: white and light grey with black text and blue accents. No logos other than the small Perplexity arrow mark and the app-grid icon. Lighting is flat, no overlay.

**Design**

Background: Split background: left half solid #F2F2F2 light grey, right half solid #FFFFFF white. Two-tone background: left panel light grey #F2F2F2, right panel white #FFFFFF, split at 50% width. Title and subtitle text is grey #545454 in a MessinaModern-style transitional serif, regular weight, title about 5.6% of slide height and subtitle about 2.6%. ### Slide 87

**Text**

Generative UI design? / “screenshots of a UI for a smartphone guide to sushi” / Source: Benedict Evans / Midjourney / Benedict Evans –– February 2023 / 88

**Layout**

Two-panel slide split at the vertical centre: a light grey left half (#F2F2F2) holds the title, subtitle and source line, and a white right half (#FFFFFF) holds a square 2x2 image grid. The title sits at about 25.6% from the top, left-aligned at x 4.6%, with the subtitle below it. The source line sits at the bottom left, and the footer (author, date, page number) sits at the bottom right, right-aligned to about 95.6% of the width.

**Visuals**

One photograph-style illustration: a 2x2 grid of four AI-generated (Midjourney) images, each showing a smartphone with a sushi app interface. Top-left: an upright dark-framed phone with a cream screen panel, a dark sidebar of small sushi thumbnails (nigiri and rolls) and orange accent buttons, on a grey-green background. Top-right: a phone tilted diagonally on a tan/peach background, with a dark green screen showing a title line, orange-topped rice rolls, salmon, and leaf accents, with a brown bottom card. Bottom-left: an upright phone on a light grey background, its top showing a close-up of salmon nigiri on a wooden tray over a dark teal header, then a cream panel with swatches (red, orange, yellow, grey dots) and a grid of white cards with sushi photos. Bottom-right: a black-framed phone angled diagonally on a beige/taupe background, its cream screen showing a grid of sushi pieces (salmon, tuna, roll, egg) with small illegible labels. Screen text throughout is illegible at this size. Framing is square, the grid is evenly split into four quadrants, lighting is soft and diffuse from above, dominant colours are sage grey, tan, light grey, beige, cream and orange, and the image is boxed with a thin light edge and no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Split background: left half solid #F2F2F2 light grey and right half solid #FFFFFF white. Two-tone layout: flat light grey #F2F2F2 left panel and flat white #FFFFFF right panel with no gradients, shadows or borders on the panels. Title in a serif family close to Messina Modern Regular (MessinaModern-Regular), regular weight, colour #545454, roughly 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, #545454, roughly 2.6% of slide height, with curly quotation marks. Source and footer text in a sans-serif close to Messina Sans (regular, book and bold), #545454 for the source and #A6A6A6 for the footer, roughly 1.9% of slide height. Image has no corner radius.

**Text**

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: a light grey left panel holds the title, three text paragraphs and the source line, and a white right panel holds a 2x2 photo grid. **Visuals**

Photograph: one 2x2 grid of four fashion photographs filling a single rectangular box with no gutters, with a faint drop shadow around the box. Top-left tile: a close crop of a woman's torso and arm in a dark red wrap-style midi dress with small white dots, V-neck, puff sleeves and gathered waist, holding a small tan bag, in front of a white wall, wooden shutter and white steps; head cropped out of frame; soft daylight; dominant colours red, white, grey and brown. Top-right tile: a full-length view of a model in a red and white patterned midi dress with puff sleeves and a red belt, standing against a glass shop front with teal-tinted windows, white socks and sneakers visible, head cropped at the top; daylight; dominant colours red, white, teal. Bottom-left tile: a full-length view of a woman in a red and white floral midi dress with puff sleeves and tiered skirt, black ankle boots, standing on grey paving in front of a black-and-white geometric lattice wall; daylight; dominant colours black, white, red, grey. Bottom-right tile: a back view of a woman with a dark bob haircut in a red and white floral puff-sleeve midi dress with a tie bow at the back, against a plain mid-grey seamless backdrop; studio lighting; dominant colours red and grey. All four tiles are full-tile, uncropped except as noted, and the grid is not overlaid or tinted.

**Design**

Background: Split background: left half solid light grey #F2F2F2, right half solid white #FFFFFF. Two-tone flat layout: light grey left panel and pure white right panel with no gradients. Source line in Messina Sans Regular at about 1.9% of slide height, colour #545454. Footer in Messina Sans with Bold for the name and Book for the date and page number at about 1.9% of slide height, colour #A6A6A6. Photo grid has square tiles with a soft shadow and no rounded corners. No accent colours on the slide apart from the red of the photographs.

### Slide 89

**Text**

Re-cast a movie? / “burt reynolds as han solo at the controls of the / millennium falcon” / Source: Benedict Evans / Midjourney / Benedict Evans –– February 2023 / 90

**Layout**

Two-column split at the 50% vertical line: a light grey left panel (about 50% width) holds the title, quotation and source line, and a white right panel holds a square four-up photo grid centred in it with about 6% side margins. The title sits top-left of the text block at about 25% from the top, left-aligned at a 4.6% left margin. The source line sits bottom-left at about 91% height, and the footer (author, date, slide number) sits bottom-right on the white panel, right-aligned to about 95% of the width.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a 2x2 grid of four AI-generated (Midjourney) portrait images of the same man, a moustached, dark-haired man in 1970s-styled sci-fi cockpit costume, all seated at the controls of a spaceship cockpit. Top-left: smiling broadly, brown leather vest over cream shirt, cockpit switch panels and small square lights behind him, dim cool-blue and brown tones. Top-right: closed-mouth smile, dark vest over sage-green shirt, a golden hexagonal window glowing behind him, green-grey instrument panels at right. Bottom-left: laughing with mouth open, tan shirt with dark harness straps, red and white cockpit lights, grey control yoke in the foreground. Bottom-right: smiling, grey-streaked hair, dark vest over orange-tan shirt, starfield and warm glowing window arc above, hands on a control panel in the foreground. Each tile is a close medium shot, head and shoulders cropped at the frame edges, warm dim interior lighting with dominant colours of brown, olive, tan and dark slate with orange-gold accents. The grid is square with no gap, hard-edged, and sits on a white panel with a faint soft drop shadow. No overlays or tints.

**Design**

Background: Split background: left half solid #F2F2F2 light grey, right half solid #FFFFFF white. Two-tone flat layout: light grey #F2F2F2 left half and pure white #FFFFFF right half. Title and quotation in grey #545454 / #535353, a classic serif (MessinaModern, Regular for the title and Book for the quotation), title about 5.6% of slide height and quotation about 3.0%. Source line and footer in MessinaSans (Regular and Book), about 1.9% of slide height, in #545454 for the source and #A6A6A6 for the footer; the author name is bold. No accent colours. Photo grid has square corners, no border, and a faint shadow. No gradients.

**Layout**

Full-bleed light-grey (#F1F1F1) slide with a white horizontal band spanning the middle from about 20.5% to 86% of slide height, forming a header and footer strip in grey. The title is left-aligned at about x 20.5% in the top grey band, with the subtitle beneath it; the chart occupies the white band with a legend at far left (x 4.8%) and the plot area from about x 20.6% to 95.3%. The source line sits at bottom-left (x 20.5%, y 90.9%) and the footer 'Benedict Evans –– February 2023' with page number at bottom-right, all in the grey bottom band.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart, titled '‘Hyper-scaler’ cloud revenue ($bn)'. Y-axis: $bn, linear, range 0 to 175, labelled every 25 (0, 25, 50, 75, 100, 125, 150, 175), light dotted horizontal gridlines at each label. X-axis: years 2016 to 2022, one column per year, baseline solid grey line. Series from bottom to top: AWS (dark grey #535353), Azure (e) (mid grey #A7A7A7), GCP (red #EB4B3C). No values are printed on the bars; estimated totals (about): 2016 about 15 (AWS about 12, Azure about 3); 2017 about 27 (AWS about 17.5, Azure about 5.5, GCP about 4); 2018 about 42 (AWS about 25.5, Azure about 10, GCP about 6); 2019 about 60.5 (AWS about 35, Azure about 16.5, GCP about 9); 2020 about 84 (AWS about 45.5, Azure about 25, GCP about 13); 2021 about 119 (AWS about 62, Azure about 37, GCP about 20); 2022 about 159 (AWS about 81, Azure about 51, GCP about 27). Legend: top-left vertical list GCP (red), Azure (e) (grey), AWS (dark grey), each with a small square swatch, stacked in reverse order to the bars. Thin white separators between stacked segments. No highlighted points. Columns about 4.3% of slide width wide, evenly spaced. No photos, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background treatment: flat light grey #F1F1F1 top and bottom bands with a flat white #FFFFFF middle band. Title colour dark grey #545454, subtitle same grey #545454. Chart text dark grey #535353 and #545454. Accent colour red #EB4B3C for GCP; mid grey #A7A7A7 for Azure; dark grey #535353 for AWS. Footer text light grey #A6A6A6. Typefaces: title and subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular, a transitional serif, regular weight, title about 5.6% of slide height (cap height about 4%), subtitle about 2.6%. Chart and labels in MessinaSans-Regular, a sans-serif, about 1.85% of slide height; chart title in MessinaSans-SemiBold about 2.2%; footer name in MessinaSans-Bold, date in MessinaSans-Book. Lines: dotted grey gridlines (#A7A7A7 approx.), solid baseline grey #8C8C8C about 0.1% thick. Shapes have square corners, no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 91

**Text**

Three views of generative AI
Silicon Valley is still trying to work out what to think about this
This is a second / wave of machine / learning
This changes / how we create / software
This takes us to / AGI / (minority view)
Benedict Evans –– February 2023 92

**Layout**

Left-aligned title and subtitle sit at the top with the title starting at about x 20.5%, and the subtitle directly beneath it. Three equal circles sit in a single horizontal row across the middle of the slide, separated by two right-pointing arrows, and the row is centred roughly between x 19.5% and 96%. The footer line sits at the bottom right with the author name, date and page number in one line, and there is no visible grid, logo or column structure.

**Visuals**

A three-step horizontal diagram. Circle 1 (dark grey #535353) is connected by a grey right-pointing block arrow (#A7A7A7) to circle 2 (dark grey #535353), which is connected by a second grey right-pointing block arrow (#A7A7A7) to circle 3 (red #EB483F). Each circle is a flat filled disc with no outline or shadow and contains centred white serif text. The arrows are flat block arrows with a rectangular shaft and triangular head, with no outline or shadow. No photographs, icons or logos are present.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat, minimal design on a plain light grey background with no gradients, shadows or textures. Title and subtitle are in dark grey #545454 serif, with the title in regular weight at about 5.6% of slide height (capitals about 3.9%) and the subtitle in regular weight at about 2.6% of slide height. Circle text is white #FFFFFF serif regular at about 3.3% of slide height. The circles are #535353, #535353 and #EB483F (red accent on the final circle), and the arrows are #A7A7A7. Footer text is #A6A6A6 sans-serif, with the author name in bold and the date and page number in book weight, at about 1.85% of slide height.

### Slide 92

**Text**

Conclusion
Benedict Evans –– February 2023
93

**Layout**

Single full-bleed dark slate slide with no body content. A translucent lighter panel spans from about 17% to 95% of the width and from about 20% to 82% of the height, with a thin white rule along its bottom edge. The title 'Conclusion' sits top-left inside the panel, left-aligned at about 20% of the width. The footer runs right-aligned along the bottom at about 91% of the height: bold author name, then the date, then the page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

none (no photographs, charts, icons or logos; the only non-text marks are the translucent panel and the thin white horizontal rule, described under elements)

**Design**

Background: Solid slate #36494E covering the whole slide. Flat background in slate #36494E with no gradient or texture. The inset panel is white #FFFFFF at 4% opacity over the slate, so it reads as a slightly lighter slate block. Title in white #FFFFFF, set in a serif (Messina Modern Regular) at roughly 9.3% of slide height, with no capitals or letter-spacing change. Footer in grey #A6A6A6 in a sans-serif (Messina Sans), about 1.85% of slide height: author name in Bold, date and page number in Book. Accent is the white rule. Corners are square, no shadows, no rounded shapes.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#36494E]: full-slide background fill (solid flat fill, covers the entire slide, no border)
- shape at x 17.3, y 20.4, w 78.1, h 61.1 [#FFFFFF]: inset translucent panel (white fill at 4% opacity over the background, square corners, no border or shadow; the title sits inside its top-left area)

94

**Layout**

Single-column title block sits top-left at about 20.5% from the left edge, with the title at roughly 5.6% from the top and the subtitle beneath it. A cluster of about 30 circles (bubbles) fills the middle band from roughly 22% to 94% horizontally and 23% to 87% vertically, with no grid. The footer line (Benedict Evans, dash, February 2023, page number 94) sits bottom-right at about 91% from the top, right-aligned to about 96% from the left.

**Visuals**

Bubble chart with no axes, no legend and no printed values. About 30 circles, each filled pale red #EB483F at 50% opacity with a white outline, except two solid red circles (China and The end of Moore’s Law) that are highlighted. Labelled bubbles: Bio, Regulation, Payments, On-demand, China, Anti-trust, The end of Moore’s Law, Supply chain decoupling, The Cookie Apocalypse, AVs, Cloud & SaaS, Privacy, Twitter. Unlabelled bubbles of various sizes are scattered around the cluster, from about 2% to 11% of slide width. Circles are flat with no shadows or gradients and overlap only slightly.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1. Light grey background #F1F1F1. Title and subtitle in dark grey #545454, MessinaModern-Regular serif, regular weight; title is large, subtitle is small. Bubble labels are white #FFFFFF MessinaModern-Regular serif, regular weight. Bubble fills are #EB483F at 50% opacity (pale red) with the two highlighted bubbles solid #EB483F. Circles have a thin white outline. Footer in grey #A6A6A6 MessinaSans: Benedict Evans in Bold, the rest in Book. Title text height about 5.6% of slide height; body and labels about 3% of slide height; footer about 1.85%.

### Slide 94

**Text**

The end of the American internet
India and China are now almost half of global mobile traffic
Mobile data traffic
RoW

China
Europe, NA, SK & JP
100%
75%
50%
25%
0%
2012
2022
Source: Ericsson, CNNIC, Benedict Evans
Benedict Evans –– February 2023
95

**Layout**

Two-tier band layout: a light grey header band (top about 20.5%) holds the serif title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.5%, and a light grey footer band (below about 86.1%) holds the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. The white content area between them contains a chart with a bold-free chart title at top-left, a legend stacked at the far left edge (x about 4.2%), a y-axis of percentage labels at x about 17 to 20%, and two stacked columns sitting on a solid baseline. The page number 95 sits at the right margin, x 94.3%.

**Visuals**

Chart: 100% stacked column chart titled 'Mobile data traffic'. Y-axis from 0% to 100% in 25% steps with labels 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%; gridlines are light grey dotted horizontal lines at each step and a solid grey baseline at 0%. X-axis categories 2012 and 2022, no axis units printed. Legend top to bottom: RoW (#A7A7A7 grey square), India (#F8DBD8 pale pink square), China (#EB483F red square), Europe, NA, SK & JP (#535353 dark grey square). Series values are not printed; estimates from bar heights. 2012 column, bottom to top: Europe, NA, SK & JP about 73%; China about 10%; India about 2%; RoW about 15%. 2022 column, bottom to top: Europe, NA, SK & JP about 25%; China about 27%; India about 21%; RoW about 27%. Columns are about 15% of slide width each, with white thin separators between segments. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands with a white content band between them. Title in MessinaModern-Regular serif, dark grey #545454, large; subtitle in same serif, #545454, smaller. Chart title and all chart labels in MessinaSans sans-serif, grey #545554 for axis labels and #535353 for legend labels. Footer author name in MessinaSans-Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in MessinaSans-Book #A6A6A6. Title size about 5.6% of slide height, body and labels about 1.9%. Accent palette: #EB483F red, #F8DBD8 pale pink, #A7A7A7 grey, #535353 charcoal. Flat shapes, no corner radii, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 95

**Text**

Old and new ‘big tech’

**Layout**

Two grey bands frame a white content panel: a light grey header band across the top about 20% of the slide height carries the title and subtitle, and a light grey footer band across the bottom carries the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. The title and subtitle are left-aligned on a shared left edge at about 20.5% of slide width, the chart title sits below them on the same edge, and the legend hangs in the left margin at about 5% of width. The chart plot runs from about 20.6% to 95.3% of width, with the source line and footer aligned to the same left and right edges.

**Visuals**

Chart: multi-series line chart with smoothed lines, titled 'Share of Fortune 100 revenue'. Y axis: 'Share of Fortune 100 revenue' in percent, ticks 0.0%, 2.5%, 5.0%, 7.5%, 10.0%, 12.5%, range 0 to 12.5%. X axis: years 1960 to about 2022, labelled every decade from 1960 to 2020. Gridlines: dotted light grey horizontal lines at each 2.5% step; solid mid grey baseline at 0.0%. Legend at left, top to bottom: General Motors (dark grey line), IBM (mid grey line), Microsoft (very light grey line), Apple (red line), Alphabet (pale pink line). Series values are printed nowhere on the slide, so estimates are marked about: General Motors starts about 8.8% in 1960, peaks about 10.8% around 1966, falls to about 5.2% around 1981, rises to about 8.6% around 1987, falls to about 2.0% around 2010 and ends about 1.2% around 2022. IBM starts about 1.0% in 1960, rises to about 4.3% around 1987, drops to about 2.6% around 1995, and ends about 0.7% around 2022. Microsoft starts about 0.5% in 2000 and ends about 1.7% around 2022. Apple starts about 0.5% in 2009, rises to about 3.1% around 2016, and ends about 3.5% around 2022. Alphabet starts about 0.4% in 2011 and ends about 2.5% around 2022. No points are highlighted with markers; the Apple line is the only red element.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-bleed, with a white #FFFFFF panel from about 20.5% to 86.1% of height. Flat, minimal editorial style. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1 and the content panel is white #FFFFFF. Title colour is dark grey #545454, subtitle the same grey in serif. Chart title and body text are dark grey #545454 in a sans-serif, with footer text light grey #A6A6A6. Accent colour is red #E8412F used only for the Apple line. Series colours are General Motors #545454, IBM #A6A6A6, Microsoft #D9D9D9, Apple #E8412F, Alphabet #F4C7C3. Title in MessinaModern-Regular serif, about 5.6% of slide height for capitals and about 5.6% em size; subtitle serif about 2.6% em; body and axis sans-serif (MessinaSans) about 1.85% em, chart title SemiBold about 2.2% em. Lines are thin, about 0.3% of slide height for series, gridlines dotted hairline. No shapes have corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Two-band header and footer on a light grey field, with a white content band across the middle. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at about x 20.4%, sitting in the top grey band. The line chart occupies the white band, from about x 17% to 96% and y 27% to 83%, with its y-axis labels at the left and x-axis labels along the bottom. The source line sits at left in the footer band; the author, date and page number sit right-aligned in the same band.

**Visuals**

Line chart with markers. Title: Average annual household automobile miles for shopping, USA. Y-axis: 0 to 4,000 (units: miles per household per year, unlabelled on the slide), gridlines every 1,000. X-axis: 1960 to 2020, labelled every 10 years, no unit. One series, drawn as a smoothed red line with filled circular markers at nine data points. Values are not printed on the slide; estimates: about 1961 842 miles; about 1969 916; about 1977 1,195; about 1983 1,555; about 1990 2,170; about 1995 2,800; about 2001 3,060; about 2009 2,970; about 2017 2,610. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the uniform markers; the 2001 peak is the highest marker. Gridlines are thin dotted grey lines at each 1,000 step; the x-axis baseline is a thin solid grey rule at 0.

**Design**

Background is light grey #F1F1F1 for the header and footer bands, with a pure white #FFFFFF content band between them. Title in a roman serif (Messina Modern, close to a Didone-style serif), regular weight, colour #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title and all axis and footer text in a geometric grotesque sans (Messina Sans), about 1.9% of slide height; chart title semibold #545554; axis labels regular #535353; footer source #545454; author name bold #A6A6A6; date and page number book weight #A6A6A6. Data line red about #EE4035, approximately 3 px stroke; markers filled red #EE4035 of similar colour. Gridlines dotted light grey about #A6A6A6 hairlines. Axis baseline solid grey about #888888. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Full-width layout with a grey #F1F1F1 header band across the top, a white central panel from about 20.5% to 86.1% of the slide height holding the chart, and a grey #F1F1F1 footer band below. The title sits left-aligned at x 20.5%, with the subtitle beneath it at the same left edge. The chart is a line chart with its y-axis labels at about x 17% to 19% and its plot area from about x 20.5% to 95%. A legend sits at the far left at x 4.8%. The source line sits at x 20.5% in the footer, and the author, date and page number are right-aligned in the footer band, ending at about x 95.6%.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'Annual US revenue ($bn, 2022 dollars)'. Y-axis: 0 to 1,000 ($bn, 2022 dollars), labelled 0, 250, 500, 750, 1,000, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled value. X-axis: years 2000 to about 2021, labelled 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015, 2020, with a thin grey baseline at y = 0. Series 1, 'Ecommerce': solid red line about 3px wide, smoothed. Values about 45 in 2000, about 75 in 2003, about 135 in 2005, about 190 in 2007, about 200 in 2009, about 225 in 2010, about 320 in 2013, about 400 in 2015, about 500 in 2017, about 600 in 2019, about 870 in 2020, about 960 in 2021 (end point, rightmost). Series 2, 'Digital ads': dotted red line, about 10 in 2000, about 30 in 2010, about 75 in 2015, about 150 in 2020, about 180 in 2021. No values are printed; all values are estimates. Legend at upper left of the plot: red solid line sample labelled 'Ecommerce' above a red dot sample labelled 'Digital ads'. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF panel from about 20.5% to 86.1% of the height. Clean, minimal editorial look. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1 with a white #FFFFFF content panel between them. Title in a serif modern face (Messina Modern Regular), dark grey #545454, large. Subtitle in the same serif, #545454, smaller. Chart title and axis labels in a sans-serif (Messina Sans, Regular/SemiBold), dark grey #545454 and #535353. Footer author name in Messina Sans Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in Messina Sans Book #A6A6A6. Source line in Messina Sans Regular #545454. Accent colour is red #E8453C for both data series. Gridlines are thin dotted grey. Title text about 5.6% of slide height; body and axis text about 1.85% of slide height. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Two horizontal bands frame the slide: a light grey #F1F1F1 header band from the top to about 20.5% of the height, a white content band from 20.5% to about 86.1%, and a light grey footer band below. The title sits top-left at x 20.5% on the header band, with its subtitle directly beneath it. The chart fills the white band, with a legend in the left column at x 4.8% and the plot starting at x about 20.5% and ending at about 95.3%. The source line sits at x 20.5% in the footer, and the author, date and page number sit right-aligned in the footer ending at about 95.2%.

**Visuals**

Chart: smooth-line time-series chart titled 'Annual US revenue ($bn, 2022 dollars)' with the title left-aligned above the plot at y 27.4%. Y-axis: '$bn, 2022 dollars', labelled 0 to 8,000 in steps of 1,000, with horizontal dotted light-grey gridlines at each 1,000 step; the 0 baseline is a solid thin grey rule. X-axis: years labelled 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015 and 2020, spanning 2000 to about 2021. No values are printed on the data series, so all values are estimates marked about. Series 1, 'Retail & food service', dark grey #4A4A4A solid line about 3.5 pt: about 5,450 in 2000, about 5,500 in 2001 to 2002, about 5,900 in 2005, about 6,050 in 2006 to 2007, about 5,450 low in 2009, about 5,900 in 2011, about 6,200 in 2013, about 6,500 in 2014 to 2015, about 6,800 in 2018, about 7,000 peak in 2019, about 6,700 dip in 2020, and about 7,450 in 2021. Series 2, 'Ecommerce', red #E8453C solid line about 3.5 pt: about 50 in 2000, about 200 in 2008 to 2009, about 400 in 2015, about 650 in 2019, about 850 in 2020, and about 950 in 2021. Series 3, 'Digital ads', red #E8453C dotted line about 2 pt: about 0 in 2000, about 50 in 2014, and about 200 in 2021. Legend: three entries stacked top-left with short line swatches, dark grey for Retail & food service, red for Ecommerce, and a red dot for Digital ads. No highlighted points or annotations.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-slide background, with a white #FFFFFF band from 20.5% to 86.1% of the height. Light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF content band. Title text is dark grey #545454 in a serif modern face (MessinaModern-Regular, regular weight), about 5.6% of slide height for the title and about 2.6% for the subtitle. Chart title and axis labels are dark grey #545454 to #535353 in a geometric sans (MessinaSans-SemiBold for the chart title, MessinaSans-Regular for labels), about 1.9% to 2.2% of slide height. Accent colour is red #E8453C for the Ecommerce and Digital ads series. Gridlines are light grey dotted hairlines. Footer text is grey #A6A6A6 (MessinaSans-Bold for the author name, MessinaSans-Book for date and page number), about 1.9% of slide height. Source line is dark grey #545454 in MessinaSans-Regular. No shadows, gradients, corner radii or rounded shapes.

**Layout**

Full-width light grey band (#F1F1F1) covers the whole slide, with a white content panel from about 20.5% to 86.1% of slide height. The title sits top-left at x 20.5%, with the subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a shared left edge. The chart occupies the white panel from x about 4.8% to 95%, with the legend stacked at top-left of the panel and the y-axis labels at x about 16.6% to 19%. The source line sits bottom-left at x 20.5% in the grey footer band, and the author, date and page number sit bottom-right in the same band, right edge at about 95.5%.

**Visuals**

One line chart titled 'Annual US revenue ($bn, 2022 dollars)'. Type: line chart with smoothed curves, four series. Y axis: linear, labelled 0 to 17,500 in steps of 2,500 (0, 2,500, 5,000, 7,500, 10,000, 12,500, 15,000, 17,500), unit $bn in 2022 dollars, faint dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled step. X axis: years 2000 to about 2021, labelled 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015, 2020, a thin solid grey baseline at 0. Series (values are estimates read from the curves, marked about, as no values are printed): Personal Consumption, mid-grey #A6A6A6 solid line, about 11,500 in 2000, about 13,600 in 2007, dip to about 13,400 in 2008, about 14,300 in 2013, about 16,600 in 2019, dip to about 15,800 in 2020, about 16,500 in 2021. Retail & food service, dark grey #545454 solid line, about 5,400 in 2000, about 6,000 in 2008, dip to about 5,450 in 2009, about 6,200 in 2013, about 7,000 in 2019, dip to about 6,700 in 2020, about 7,400 in 2021. Ecommerce, red #E8453C solid line, about 50 in 2000, about 300 in 2010, about 650 in 2019, about 900 in 2020, about 1,000 in 2021. Digital ads, red dotted line with small dots, near 0 in 2000, about 200 in 2021. Legend at top-left of chart: grey line swatch for Personal Consumption, dark grey line swatch for Retail & food service, red line swatch for Ecommerce, a single red dot for Digital ads. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 with a white #FFFFFF content panel behind the chart. Minimal, flat, two-tone background. Title in MessinaModern-Regular serif, colour #545454, large. Subtitle in the same serif, smaller, #545454. Chart title in MessinaSans-SemiBold sans-serif, #545554. Axis labels and legend in MessinaSans-Regular sans-serif, #545554 and #535353. Footer text in MessinaSans, #545454 for source and #A6A6A6 for author and date and page number, with the author name in Bold and date in Book. Accent colour is red #E8453C used only for the Ecommerce and Digital ads series. Line strokes are about 2-3 px; gridlines dotted, light grey. No shadows, gradients or corner radii.

**Layout**

Three horizontal bands: a light grey header band (top 20.5%) holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.5%, a white content panel (20.5% to 86.1%) holding a single full-width stacked bar chart with its chart title at top-left and a legend at far left, and a light grey footer band (below 86.1%) holding the source line at left (x 20.5%) and the author, date and page number right-aligned in the footer's right area. Left margin of content is about 20.5%; right margin of the chart runs to about 95.3%; page number sits at about x 94.1%.

**Visuals**

Stacked vertical bar chart titled 'Satellites launched'. One bar per year, approximately 1950 to 2022, bars separated by thin white gaps. Y axis: number of satellites launched, linear, range 0 to 2,500, labelled ticks at 0, 500, 1,000, 1,500, 2,000, 2,500, with light dotted grey horizontal gridlines at each labelled tick. X axis: years, labelled every decade from 1950 to 2020, no unit. Series 1 'Other' (dark grey #545454) at bottom; series 2 'Starlink' (red #E84B3C) stacked on top. Printed values: none; estimates are approximate. Approximate values: 1950 to 1958 about 0; 1960 about 25; 1965 about 150; 1970 about 130; 1980 about 150; 1990 about 150; 1997 about 150; 1998 Other about 470 and no Starlink; 2000 about 120; 2005 about 75; 2010 about 120; 2013 about 205; 2017 about 380; 2018 about 380; 2019 Other about 290 and Starlink about 130 (total about 420); 2020 Other about 410 and Starlink about 820 (total about 1,230); 2021 Other about 730 and Starlink about 990 (total about 1,720); 2022 Other about 560 and Starlink about 1,690 (total about 2,250). Legend: at far left, top entry red square 'Starlink', below it dark grey square 'Other'. Highlighted points: none marked; the 1998 Other spike and the 2019 to 2022 Starlink stacks are the tallest visible features.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-slide fill, with a white #FFFFFF panel across the middle band. Flat, minimal, no gradients or shadows. Header and footer bands #F1F1F1 light grey; content panel white #FFFFFF. Title and subtitle in MessinaModern-Regular, a transitional serif, colour #545454 charcoal grey, title about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%. Chart title and axis labels in MessinaSans, a grotesque sans-serif: chart title SemiBold #545554 at about 2.2%, axis and legend labels Regular #545454 at about 1.9%. Accent colour is red #E84B3C used only for Starlink. Bars are solid with no outline and thin white separators; gridlines are thin dotted grey #A6A6A6-like lines. Source line Regular #545454 at about 1.9%; author name Bold #A6A6A6 and date and page number Book #A6A6A6 at about 1.9%.

### Slide 101

**Text**

About / Benedict Evans –– February 2023 / 102

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark slate slide with a single large lighter panel covering the right and centre of the slide, inset from the left edge. The title 'About' sits top-left inside the panel, left-aligned, with empty space below it. A white rule runs along the bottom edge of the panel. The footer line is right-aligned at the bottom right, outside the panel, with the page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

none. The only non-text element is a thin white horizontal rule along the bottom edge of the panel, spanning its full width.
